## The 543 Places the Critical Text and the Received Text Differ

This is the appendix to [Why Your NT is Missing Some Verses](/nkjv/). Every place in the New Testament where a critical-text Greek edition and the Received Text part ways — **543 verses** — set side by side, verse by verse.

The **CT** line is what a critical-text Bible prints; the **TR** line is the Received Text. The **bold, underlined** words are what changed. Hover a reference for the manuscript evidence, Greek and all.

The English follows the NKJV’s own idiom: the CT line reads the way an NKJV-style critical text would put it, the TR line what the NKJV itself prints.<link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles/manuscript.css"><div class="trt-legend">
<button type="button" class="trt-btn trt-swap" data-a="CT" data-b="TR" data-label="Reading order" aria-pressed="true"><span class="sw-ico" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="sw-txt"><span class="sw-a">TR</span><span class="sw-ar" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="sw-b">CT</span></span></button>
<span class="lg cr">Critical Text</span>
<span class="lg tr">Received Text (NKJV)</span>

<span class="lg hint">hover a verse — or tap on a phone — for the evidence</span>
</div>

<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds the fulfilment formula and Psalm 22:18 quotation" data-ref="Matthew 27:35"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 27:35</span><span class="srnote">TR adds the fulfilment formula and Psalm 22:18 quotation</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And they crucified Him, and divided His garments, casting <mark class="chg">lots, that it might be fulfilled which was spoken by the prophet: They divided My garments among them, and for My clothing they cast</mark> lots.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And they crucified Him, and divided His garments, casting lots.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Ἰωσῆ, critical Ἰωσήφ; Korean 요셉 is the critical name" data-ref="Matthew 27:56"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 27:56</span><span class="srnote">TR Ἰωσῆ, critical Ἰωσήφ; Korean 요셉 is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Among them were Mary Magdalene, Mary the mother of James and <mark class="chg">Joses,</mark> and the mother of Zebedee's sons.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Among them were Mary Magdalene, Mary the mother of James and <mark class="chg">Joseph,</mark> and the mother of Zebedee's sons.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds νυκτός (by night)" data-ref="Matthew 27:64"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 27:64</span><span class="srnote">TR adds νυκτός (by night)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore command that the tomb be made secure until the third day, lest His disciples come <mark class="chg">by night</mark> and steal Him away and say to the people that He has risen from the dead, and the last deception be worse than the first.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore command that the tomb be made secure until the third day, lest His disciples come and steal Him away and say to the people that He has risen from the dead, and the last deception be worse than the first.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἀπὸ τῆς θύρας (from the door)" data-ref="Matthew 28:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 28:2</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἀπὸ τῆς θύρας (from the door)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And behold, there was a great earthquake, for an angel of the Lord descended from heaven and came and rolled back the stone <mark class="chg">from the door</mark> and sat on it.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And behold, there was a great earthquake, for an angel of the Lord descended from heaven and came and rolled back the stone and sat on it.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὁ κύριος (the Lord)" data-ref="Matthew 28:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 28:6</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὁ κύριος (the Lord)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He is not here, for He has risen, as He said. Come, see the place where <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> lay.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He is not here, for He has risen, as He said. Come, see the place where <mark class="chg">He</mark> lay.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὡς δὲ ἐπορεύοντο ἀπαγγεῖλαι τοῖς μαθηταῖς αὐτοῦ" data-ref="Matthew 28:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 28:9</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὡς δὲ ἐπορεύοντο ἀπαγγεῖλαι τοῖς μαθηταῖς αὐτοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And <mark class="chg">as they went to tell His disciples,</mark> behold, Jesus met them, saying, Rejoice! And they came and held Him by the feet and worshiped Him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And behold, Jesus met them, saying, Rejoice! And they came and held Him by the feet and worshiped Him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes with ἀμήν" data-ref="Matthew 28:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Matthew 28:20</span><span class="srnote">TR closes with ἀμήν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">teaching them to observe all things that I have commanded you; and behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">teaching them to observe all things that I have commanded you; and behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Mark</span><span class="bkcount">46</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐν τοῖς προφήταις, not ἐν τῷ Ἠσαΐᾳ" data-ref="Mark 1:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 1:2</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐν τοῖς προφήταις, not ἐν τῷ Ἠσαΐᾳ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">As it is written in the <mark class="chg">prophets:</mark> Behold, I send My messenger before Your face, who will prepare Your way before You.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">As it is written in <mark class="chg">Isaiah</mark> the <mark class="chg">prophet:</mark> Behold, I send My messenger before Your face, who will prepare Your way before You.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds τῆς βασιλείας (of the kingdom)" data-ref="Mark 1:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 1:14</span><span class="srnote">TR adds τῆς βασιλείας (of the kingdom)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now after John was put in prison, Jesus came to Galilee, preaching the gospel of <mark class="chg">the kingdom of</mark> God,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now after John was put in prison, Jesus came to Galilee, preaching the gospel of God,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καὶ πίνει (and drinks)" data-ref="Mark 2:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 2:16</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καὶ πίνει (and drinks)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when the scribes and Pharisees saw Him eating with the tax collectors and sinners, they said to His disciples, How is it that He eats <mark class="chg">and drinks</mark> with tax collectors and sinners?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when the scribes and Pharisees saw Him eating with the tax collectors and sinners, they said to His disciples, How is it that He eats with tax collectors and sinners?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds εἰς μετάνοιαν (to repentance)" data-ref="Mark 2:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 2:17</span><span class="srnote">TR adds εἰς μετάνοιαν (to repentance)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">When Jesus heard it, He said to them, Those who are well have no need of a physician, but those who are sick. I did not come to call the righteous, but <mark class="chg">sinners, to repentance.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">When Jesus heard it, He said to them, Those who are well have no need of a physician, but those who are sick. I did not come to call the righteous, but <mark class="chg">sinners.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR οἱ Φαρισαῖοι, not οἱ μαθηταὶ τῶν Φαρισαίων" data-ref="Mark 2:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 2:18</span><span class="srnote">TR οἱ Φαρισαῖοι, not οἱ μαθηταὶ τῶν Φαρισαίων</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The disciples of John and of the Pharisees were fasting. Then they came and said to Him, Why do the disciples of John and the Pharisees fast, but Your disciples do not fast?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The disciples of John and of the Pharisees were fasting. Then they came and said to Him, Why do the disciples of John and <mark class="chg">of</mark> the Pharisees fast, but Your disciples do not fast?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὑγιὴς ὡς ἡ ἄλλη (whole as the other)" data-ref="Mark 3:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 3:5</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὑγιὴς ὡς ἡ ἄλλη (whole as the other)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when He had looked around at them with anger, being grieved by the hardness of their hearts, He said to the man, Stretch out your hand. And he stretched it out, and his hand was <mark class="chg">restored as whole as the other.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when He had looked around at them with anger, being grieved by the hardness of their hearts, He said to the man, Stretch out your hand. And he stretched it out, and his hand was <mark class="chg">restored.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds θεραπεύειν τὰς νόσους (heal sicknesses)" data-ref="Mark 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 3:15</span><span class="srnote">TR adds θεραπεύειν τὰς νόσους (heal sicknesses)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and to have power to <mark class="chg">heal sicknesses and to</mark> cast out demons.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and to have power to cast out demons.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR κρίσεως (judgment), not ἁμαρτήματος (sin)" data-ref="Mark 3:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 3:29</span><span class="srnote">TR κρίσεως (judgment), not ἁμαρτήματος (sin)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but he who blasphemes against the Holy Spirit never has forgiveness, but is <mark class="chg">subject to</mark> eternal <mark class="chg">condemnation.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but he who blasphemes against the Holy Spirit never has forgiveness, but is <mark class="chg">guilty of an</mark> eternal <mark class="chg">sin.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τὰ πετεινὰ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ (birds of the air); 개역개정 has 새들 only, cf. 4:32 공중의 새들" data-ref="Mark 4:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 4:4</span><span class="srnote">TR τὰ πετεινὰ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ (birds of the air); 개역개정 has 새들 only, cf. 4:32 공중의 새들</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And it happened, as he sowed, that some seed fell by the wayside; and the birds <mark class="chg">of the air</mark> came and devoured it.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And it happened, as he sowed, that some seed fell by the wayside; and the birds came and devoured it.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐν ταῖς καρδίαις αὐτῶν (sown in their hearts), not CT εἰς αὐτούς (그들에게)" data-ref="Mark 4:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 4:15</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐν ταῖς καρδίαις αὐτῶν (sown in their hearts), not CT εἰς αὐτούς (그들에게)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And these are the ones by the wayside where the word is sown. When they hear, Satan comes immediately and takes away the word that was sown in <mark class="chg">their hearts.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And these are the ones by the wayside where the word is sown. When they hear, Satan comes immediately and takes away the word that was sown in <mark class="chg">them.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Γαδαρηνῶν, critical Γερασηνῶν; Korean 거라사인 is the critical name" data-ref="Mark 5:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 5:1</span><span class="srnote">TR Γαδαρηνῶν, critical Γερασηνῶν; Korean 거라사인 is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then they came to the other side of the sea, to the country of the <mark class="chg">Gadarenes.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then they came to the other side of the sea, to the country of the <mark class="chg">Gerasenes.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Ἰωσῆ, critical Ἰωσήφ; Korean 요셉 is the critical name" data-ref="Mark 6:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 6:3</span><span class="srnote">TR Ἰωσῆ, critical Ἰωσήφ; Korean 요셉 is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Is this not the carpenter, the Son of Mary, and brother of James and <mark class="chg">Joses,</mark> Judas, and Simon? And are not His sisters here with us? So they were offended at Him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Is this not the carpenter, the Son of Mary, and brother of James and <mark class="chg">Joseph,</mark> Judas, and Simon? And are not His sisters here with us? So they were offended at Him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds the Sodom/Gomorrah saying (ἀμὴν λέγω ὑμῖν … ἢ τῇ πόλει ἐκείνῃ), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 6:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 6:11</span><span class="srnote">TR adds the Sodom/Gomorrah saying (ἀμὴν λέγω ὑμῖν … ἢ τῇ πόλει ἐκείνῃ), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And whoever will not receive you nor hear you, when you depart from there, shake off the dust under your feet as a testimony against them. <mark class="chg">Assuredly, I say to you, it will be more tolerable for Sodom and Gomorrah in the day of judgment than for that city.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And whoever will not receive you nor hear you, when you depart from there, shake off the dust under your feet as a testimony against them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR πολλὰ ἐποίει (&#x27;did many things&#x27;), not the critical ἠπόρει (&#x27;was perplexed&#x27;)" data-ref="Mark 6:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 6:20</span><span class="srnote">TR πολλὰ ἐποίει ('did many things'), not the critical ἠπόρει ('was perplexed')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for Herod feared John, knowing that he was a just and holy man, and he protected him. And when he heard him, he <mark class="chg">did many things,</mark> and heard him gladly.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for Herod feared John, knowing that he was a just and holy man, and he protected him. And when he heard him, he <mark class="chg">was greatly perplexed,</mark> and <mark class="chg">yet he</mark> heard him gladly.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds βαπτισμοὺς ξεστῶν καὶ ποτηρίων καὶ ἄλλα παρόμοια τοιαῦτα πολλά (washings of vessels and many like things)" data-ref="Mark 7:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 7:8</span><span class="srnote">TR adds βαπτισμοὺς ξεστῶν καὶ ποτηρίων καὶ ἄλλα παρόμοια τοιαῦτα πολλά (washings of vessels and many like things)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For laying aside the commandment of God, you hold the tradition of <mark class="chg">men, such as the washing of pitchers and cups, and many other such things you do.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For laying aside the commandment of God, you hold the tradition of <mark class="chg">men.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR 7:15 closes with ἀλλὰ τὰ ἐκπορευόμενα ἀπ᾿ αὐτοῦ, ἐκεῖνά ἐστι τὰ κοινοῦντα τὸν ἄνθρωπον; 개역개정 moved this clause into v.16" data-ref="Mark 7:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 7:15</span><span class="srnote">TR 7:15 closes with ἀλλὰ τὰ ἐκπορευόμενα ἀπ᾿ αὐτοῦ, ἐκεῖνά ἐστι τὰ κοινοῦντα τὸν ἄνθρωπον; 개역개정 moved this clause into v.16</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">There is nothing that enters a man from outside which can defile <mark class="chg">him; but the things which come out of him, those are the things that defile a man.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">There is nothing that enters a man from outside which can defile <mark class="chg">him.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR 7:16 is the ears-to-hear saying (Εἴ τις ἔχει ὦτα ἀκούειν ἀκουέτω), which 개역개정 replaced with the clause from 7:15b; that clause is restored in the 7:15 patch" data-ref="Mark 7:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 7:16</span><span class="srnote">TR 7:16 is the ears-to-hear saying (Εἴ τις ἔχει ὦτα ἀκούειν ἀκουέτω), which 개역개정 replaced with the clause from 7:15b; that clause is restored in the 7:15 patch</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">If anyone has ears to hear, let him hear!</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">That which comes out of a man, that defiles a man.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR εἰς τὰ μεθόρια Τύρου καὶ Σιδῶνος; 개역개정 dropped 시돈" data-ref="Mark 7:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 7:24</span><span class="srnote">TR εἰς τὰ μεθόρια Τύρου καὶ Σιδῶνος; 개역개정 dropped 시돈</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">From there He arose and went to the region of <mark class="chg">Tyre and Sidon.</mark> And He entered a house and wanted no one to know it, but He could not be hidden.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">From there He arose and went to the region of <mark class="chg">Tyre.</mark> And He entered a house and wanted no one to know it, but He could not be hidden.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds μηδὲ εἴπῃς τινὶ ἐν τῇ κώμῃ (nor tell anyone in the town)" data-ref="Mark 8:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 8:26</span><span class="srnote">TR adds μηδὲ εἴπῃς τινὶ ἐν τῇ κώμῃ (nor tell anyone in the town)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then He sent him away to his house, saying, Neither go into the <mark class="chg">town, nor tell anyone in the</mark> town.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then He sent him away to his house, saying, Neither go into the town.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR λευκὰ λίαν ὡς χιών (as snow); 개역개정 dropped the simile" data-ref="Mark 9:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:3</span><span class="srnote">TR λευκὰ λίαν ὡς χιών (as snow); 개역개정 dropped the simile</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">His clothes became shining, exceedingly white, <mark class="chg">like snow,</mark> such as no launderer on earth can whiten them.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">His clothes became shining, exceedingly white, such as no launderer on earth can whiten them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has μετὰ δακρύων (with tears) and the vocative Κύριε (주여), both absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 9:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:24</span><span class="srnote">TR has μετὰ δακρύων (with tears) and the vocative Κύριε (주여), both absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Immediately the father of the child cried out and <mark class="chg">said with tears, Lord,</mark> I believe; help my unbelief!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Immediately the father of the child cried out and <mark class="chg">said,</mark> I believe; help my unbelief!</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR: ἐν προσευχῇ καὶ νηστείᾳ -- the fasting is restored" data-ref="Mark 9:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:29</span><span class="srnote">TR: ἐν προσευχῇ καὶ νηστείᾳ -- the fasting is restored</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So He said to them, This kind can come out by nothing but <mark class="chg">prayer and fasting.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So He said to them, This kind can come out by nothing but <mark class="chg">prayer.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τῇ τρίτῃ ἡμέρᾳ (on the third day), not μετὰ τρεῖς ἡμέρας (삼 일 만에)" data-ref="Mark 9:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:31</span><span class="srnote">TR τῇ τρίτῃ ἡμέρᾳ (on the third day), not μετὰ τρεῖς ἡμέρας (삼 일 만에)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For He taught His disciples and said to them, The Son of Man is being delivered into the hands of men, and they will kill Him, and <mark class="chg">on the third day</mark> He will rise.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For He taught His disciples and said to them, The Son of Man is being delivered into the hands of men, and they will kill Him, and <mark class="chg">after three days</mark> He will rise.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (없음) in 개역개정; matches the TR 9:48 wording" data-ref="Mark 9:44"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:44</span><span class="srnote">printed (없음) in 개역개정; matches the TR 9:48 wording</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">where their worm does not die and the fire is not quenched.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds εἰς τὸ πῦρ τὸ ἄσβεστον (into the unquenchable fire); 개역개정 has only 지옥에, unlike 9:43" data-ref="Mark 9:45"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:45</span><span class="srnote">TR adds εἰς τὸ πῦρ τὸ ἄσβεστον (into the unquenchable fire); 개역개정 has only 지옥에, unlike 9:43</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And if your foot causes you to sin, cut it off. It is better for you to enter life lame, rather than having two feet, to be cast into <mark class="chg">hell.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (없음) in 개역개정; matches the TR 9:48 wording" data-ref="Mark 9:46"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:46</span><span class="srnote">printed (없음) in 개역개정; matches the TR 9:48 wording</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">where their worm does not die and the fire is not quenched.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καὶ πᾶσα θυσία ἁλὶ ἁλισθήσεται (every sacrifice shall be seasoned with salt)" data-ref="Mark 9:49"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 9:49</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καὶ πᾶσα θυσία ἁλὶ ἁλισθήσεται (every sacrifice shall be seasoned with salt)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For everyone will be seasoned with <mark class="chg">fire, and every sacrifice will be seasoned with salt.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For everyone will be seasoned with <mark class="chg">fire.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἄρας τὸν σταυρόν (take up the cross), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 10:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 10:21</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἄρας τὸν σταυρόν (take up the cross), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then Jesus, looking at him, loved him, and said to him, One thing you lack: Go your way, sell whatever you have and give to the poor, and you will have treasure in heaven; and come, <mark class="chg">take up the cross, and</mark> follow Me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then Jesus, looking at him, loved him, and said to him, One thing you lack: Go your way, sell whatever you have and give to the poor, and you will have treasure in heaven; and come, follow Me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τοὺς πεποιθότας ἐπὶ τοῖς χρήμασιν (those who trust in riches); 개역개정 omits the clause" data-ref="Mark 10:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 10:24</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τοὺς πεποιθότας ἐπὶ τοῖς χρήμασιν (those who trust in riches); 개역개정 omits the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And the disciples were astonished at His words. But Jesus answered again and said to them, Children, how hard it is <mark class="chg">for those who trust in riches</mark> to enter the kingdom of God!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And the disciples were astonished at His words. But Jesus answered again and said to them, Children, how hard it is to enter the kingdom of God!</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR list includes γυναῖκα (wife); 개역개정 list omits 아내" data-ref="Mark 10:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 10:29</span><span class="srnote">TR list includes γυναῖκα (wife); 개역개정 list omits 아내</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So Jesus answered and said, Assuredly, I say to you, there is no one who has left house or brothers or sisters or mother or father or <mark class="chg">wife or</mark> children or lands, for My sake and the gospel's,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So Jesus answered and said, Assuredly, I say to you, there is no one who has left house or brothers or sisters or mother or father or children or lands, for My sake and the gospel's,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τῇ τρίτῃ ἡμέρᾳ (on the third day), not μετὰ τρεῖς ἡμέρας (삼 일 만에)" data-ref="Mark 10:34"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 10:34</span><span class="srnote">TR τῇ τρίτῃ ἡμέρᾳ (on the third day), not μετὰ τρεῖς ἡμέρας (삼 일 만에)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and they will mock Him, and scourge Him, and spit on Him, and kill Him. And <mark class="chg">after three days</mark> He will rise again.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐκ τῶν δένδρων (from the trees); 개역개정 follows the critical text ἐκ τῶν ἀγρῶν (들에서)" data-ref="Mark 11:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 11:8</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐκ τῶν δένδρων (from the trees); 개역개정 follows the critical text ἐκ τῶν ἀγρῶν (들에서)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And many spread their clothes on the road, and others cut down leafy branches from the <mark class="chg">trees</mark> and spread them on the road.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And many spread their clothes on the road, and others cut down leafy branches from the <mark class="chg">fields</mark> and spread them on the road.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἐν ὀνόματι κυρίου (in the name of the Lord), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 11:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 11:10</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἐν ὀνόματι κυρίου (in the name of the Lord), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Blessed is the kingdom of our father David that <mark class="chg">comes in the name of the Lord!</mark> Hosanna in the highest!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Blessed is the kingdom of our father David that <mark class="chg">comes!</mark> Hosanna in the highest!</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR" data-ref="Mark 11:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 11:26</span><span class="srnote">printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">But if you do not forgive, neither will your Father in heaven forgive your trespasses.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has λιθοβολήσαντες (stoned him), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 12:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 12:4</span><span class="srnote">TR has λιθοβολήσαντες (stoned him), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Again he sent another servant to them, and they <mark class="chg">stoned him, and</mark> struck him on the head and treated him shamefully.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Again he sent another servant to them, and they struck him on the head and treated him shamefully.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the verse with αὕτη πρώτη ἐντολή (this is the first commandment), dropped in 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 12:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 12:30</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the verse with αὕτη πρώτη ἐντολή (this is the first commandment), dropped in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And you shall love the Lord your God with all your heart, with all your soul, with all your mind, and with all your strength. <mark class="chg">This is the first commandment.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And you shall love the Lord your God with all your heart, with all your soul, with all your mind, and with all your strength.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR list includes ἐξ ὅλης τῆς ψυχῆς (with all the soul); 개역개정 omits 목숨" data-ref="Mark 12:33"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 12:33</span><span class="srnote">TR list includes ἐξ ὅλης τῆς ψυχῆς (with all the soul); 개역개정 omits 목숨</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And to love Him with all the heart, with all the understanding, <mark class="chg">with all the soul, and</mark> with all the strength, and to love one's neighbor as himself, is more than all the whole burnt offerings and sacrifices.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And to love Him with all the heart, with all the understanding, with all the strength, and to love one's neighbor as himself, is more than all the whole burnt offerings and sacrifices.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has τὸ ῥηθὲν ὑπὸ Δανιὴλ τοῦ προφήτου (spoken of by Daniel the prophet), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Mark 13:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 13:14</span><span class="srnote">TR has τὸ ῥηθὲν ὑπὸ Δανιὴλ τοῦ προφήτου (spoken of by Daniel the prophet), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So when you see the abomination of <mark class="chg">desolation, spoken of by Daniel the prophet,</mark> standing where it ought not (let the reader understand), then let those who are in Judea flee to the mountains.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So when you see the abomination of <mark class="chg">desolation</mark> standing where it ought not (let the reader understand), then let those who are in Judea flee to the mountains.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἡ φυγὴ ὑμῶν (your flight); 개역개정 reduces it to 이 일" data-ref="Mark 13:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 13:18</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἡ φυγὴ ὑμῶν (your flight); 개역개정 reduces it to 이 일</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And pray that <mark class="chg">your flight</mark> may not <mark class="chg">be</mark> in winter.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And pray that <mark class="chg">it</mark> may not <mark class="chg">happen</mark> in winter.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has καὶ προσεύχεσθε (and pray); 개역개정 has only 주의하라 깨어 있으라" data-ref="Mark 13:33"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 13:33</span><span class="srnote">TR has καὶ προσεύχεσθε (and pray); 개역개정 has only 주의하라 깨어 있으라</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Take heed, <mark class="chg">watch and pray;</mark> for you do not know when the time is.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Take heed, <mark class="chg">watch;</mark> for you do not know when the time is.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has λάβετε φάγετε (take, eat); 개역개정 has only 받으라" data-ref="Mark 14:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 14:22</span><span class="srnote">TR has λάβετε φάγετε (take, eat); 개역개정 has only 받으라</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And as they were eating, Jesus took bread, blessed and broke it, and gave it to them and said, <mark class="chg">Take, eat;</mark> this is My body.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And as they were eating, Jesus took bread, blessed and broke it, and gave it to them and said, <mark class="chg">Take;</mark> this is My body.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has τῆς καινῆς διαθήκης (the new covenant); 개역개정 omits 새" data-ref="Mark 14:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 14:24</span><span class="srnote">TR has τῆς καινῆς διαθήκης (the new covenant); 개역개정 omits 새</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And He said to them, This is My blood of the <mark class="chg">new</mark> covenant, which is shed for many.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And He said to them, This is My blood of the covenant, which is shed for many.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἐν ἐμοὶ ἐν τῇ νυκτὶ ταύτῃ (because of me this night); 개역개정 omits 이 밤에" data-ref="Mark 14:27"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 14:27</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἐν ἐμοὶ ἐν τῇ νυκτὶ ταύτῃ (because of me this night); 개역개정 omits 이 밤에</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then Jesus said to them, All of you will be made to stumble because of <mark class="chg">Me this night,</mark> for it is written: I will strike the Shepherd, and the sheep will be scattered.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then Jesus said to them, All of you will be made to stumble because of <mark class="chg">Me,</mark> for it is written: I will strike the Shepherd, and the sheep will be scattered.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR οἱ νεανίσκοι ἐκράτησαν (&#x27;the young men seized him&#x27;)" data-ref="Mark 14:51"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 14:51</span><span class="srnote">TR οἱ νεανίσκοι ἐκράτησαν ('the young men seized him')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now a certain young man followed Him, having a linen cloth thrown around his naked body. <tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the Gospel with Ἀμήν; 개역개정 omits it" data-ref="Mark 16:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Mark 16:20</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the Gospel with Ἀμήν; 개역개정 omits it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And they went out and preached everywhere, the Lord working with them and confirming the word through the accompanying signs. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And they went out and preached everywhere, the Lord working with them and confirming the word through the accompanying signs.</span></span></tr>

<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ὁ ἄγγελος as subject and the clause εὐλογημένη σὺ ἐν γυναιξίν (blessed are you among women), both absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 1:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 1:28</span><span class="srnote">TR has ὁ ἄγγελος as subject and the clause εὐλογημένη σὺ ἐν γυναιξίν (blessed are you among women), both absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And <mark class="chg">the angel came</mark> in to <mark class="chg">her and</mark> said, Rejoice, highly favored one, the Lord is with <mark class="chg">you; blessed are you among women!</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And <mark class="chg">coming</mark> in to <mark class="chg">her, he</mark> said, Rejoice, highly favored one, the Lord is with <mark class="chg">you!</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἡ δὲ ἰδοῦσα (when she saw him); 개역개정 follows the critical text&#x27;s bare ἐπὶ τῷ λόγῳ with 듣고" data-ref="Luke 1:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 1:29</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἡ δὲ ἰδοῦσα (when she saw him); 개역개정 follows the critical text's bare ἐπὶ τῷ λόγῳ with 듣고</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when she <mark class="chg">saw him,</mark> she was troubled at his saying, and considered what manner of greeting this was.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when she <mark class="chg">heard this,</mark> she was troubled at his saying, and considered what manner of greeting this was.</span></span></tr>

<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads οὐκ ἔγνω Ἰωσὴφ καὶ ἡ μήτηρ αὐτοῦ; 개역개정&#x27;s 그 부모 follows the critical text&#x27;s οἱ γονεῖς" data-ref="Luke 2:43"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 2:43</span><span class="srnote">TR reads οὐκ ἔγνω Ἰωσὴφ καὶ ἡ μήτηρ αὐτοῦ; 개역개정's 그 부모 follows the critical text's οἱ γονεῖς</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">When they had finished the days, as they returned, the Boy Jesus lingered behind in Jerusalem. And His <mark class="chg">parents</mark> did not know it,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has Φιλίππου (Philip); 개역개정 drops the name, leaving only 그의 동생의 아내" data-ref="Luke 3:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 3:19</span><span class="srnote">TR has Φιλίππου (Philip); 개역개정 drops the name, leaving only 그의 동생의 아내</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But Herod the tetrarch, being rebuked by him concerning Herodias, his <mark class="chg">brother Philip's</mark> wife, and for all the evils which Herod had done,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But Herod the tetrarch, being rebuked by him concerning Herodias, his <mark class="chg">brother's</mark> wife, and for all the evils which Herod had done,</span></span></tr>

<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καὶ ἀμφότεροι συντηροῦνται (and both are preserved)" data-ref="Luke 5:38"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 5:38</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καὶ ἀμφότεροι συντηροῦνται (and both are preserved)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But new wine must be put into new <mark class="chg">wineskins, and both are preserved.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But new wine must be put into new <mark class="chg">wineskins.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR δευτεροπρώτῳ (&#x27;second-first sabbath&#x27;)" data-ref="Luke 6:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 6:1</span><span class="srnote">TR δευτεροπρώτῳ ('second-first sabbath')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now it happened on <mark class="chg">the second</mark> Sabbath <mark class="chg">after the first</mark> that He went through the grainfields. <tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὑγιὴς ὡς ἡ ἄλλη (whole as the other); cf. the Mark 3:5 patch" data-ref="Luke 6:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 6:10</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὑγιὴς ὡς ἡ ἄλλη (whole as the other); cf. the Mark 3:5 patch</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when He had looked around at them all, He said to the man, Stretch out your hand. And he did so, and his hand was <mark class="chg">restored.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR gives the reason as τεθεμελίωτο γὰρ ἐπὶ τὴν πέτραν (for it was founded on the rock); 개역개정&#x27;s 잘 지었기 때문에 follows the critical text" data-ref="Luke 6:48"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 6:48</span><span class="srnote">TR gives the reason as τεθεμελίωτο γὰρ ἐπὶ τὴν πέτραν (for it was founded on the rock); 개역개정's 잘 지었기 때문에 follows the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He is like a man building a house, who dug deep and laid the foundation on the rock. <tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads προφήτης Ἰωάννου τοῦ βαπτιστοῦ (a greater prophet than John the Baptist); 개역개정 drops 선지자 and 세례" data-ref="Luke 7:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 7:28</span><span class="srnote">TR reads προφήτης Ἰωάννου τοῦ βαπτιστοῦ (a greater prophet than John the Baptist); 개역개정 drops 선지자 and 세례</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For I say to you, among those born of women there is not a greater <mark class="chg">prophet</mark> than <mark class="chg">John the Baptist;</mark> but he who is least in the kingdom of God is greater than he.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I say to you, among those born of women there is not a greater than <mark class="chg">John;</mark> but he who is least in the kingdom of God is greater than he.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Γαδαρηνῶν, critical Γερασηνῶν; Korean 거라사인 is the critical name" data-ref="Luke 8:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 8:26</span><span class="srnote">TR Γαδαρηνῶν, critical Γερασηνῶν; Korean 거라사인 is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then they sailed to the country of the <mark class="chg">Gadarenes,</mark> which is opposite Galilee.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then they sailed to the country of the <mark class="chg">Gerasenes,</mark> which is opposite Galilee.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has καὶ οἱ μετ᾿ αὐτοῦ (Peter and those with him) and repeats the question λέγεις, Τίς ὁ ἁψάμενός μου; both dropped in 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 8:45"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 8:45</span><span class="srnote">TR has καὶ οἱ μετ᾿ αὐτοῦ (Peter and those with him) and repeats the question λέγεις, Τίς ὁ ἁψάμενός μου; both dropped in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And Jesus said, Who touched Me? <tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἐκβαλὼν ἔξω πάντας (having put them all outside); 개역개정 omits the clause" data-ref="Luke 8:54"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 8:54</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἐκβαλὼν ἔξω πάντας (having put them all outside); 개역개정 omits the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But He <mark class="chg">put them all outside,</mark> took her by the hand and called, saying, Little girl, arise.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But He took her by the hand and called, saying, Little girl, arise.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὁ ἀγαπητός (my beloved Son); 개역개정&#x27;s 택함을 받은 자 follows the critical text&#x27;s ὁ ἐκλελεγμένος" data-ref="Luke 9:35"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 9:35</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὁ ἀγαπητός (my beloved Son); 개역개정's 택함을 받은 자 follows the critical text's ὁ ἐκλελεγμένος</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And a voice came out of the cloud, saying, This is My <mark class="chg">beloved Son.</mark> Hear Him!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And a voice came out of the cloud, saying, This is My <mark class="chg">Son, the Chosen One.</mark> Hear Him!</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads καθ᾿ ἡμῶν … ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν (against us … for us); 개역개정&#x27;s 너희 follows the critical text&#x27;s ὑμῶν" data-ref="Luke 9:50"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 9:50</span><span class="srnote">TR reads καθ᾿ ἡμῶν … ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν (against us … for us); 개역개정's 너희 follows the critical text's ὑμῶν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But Jesus said to him, Do not forbid him, for he who is not against <mark class="chg">us</mark> is for <mark class="chg">us.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But Jesus said to him, Do not forbid him, for he who is not against <mark class="chg">you</mark> is for <mark class="chg">you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὡς καὶ Ἠλίας ἐποίησε (as Elijah did), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 9:54"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 9:54</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὡς καὶ Ἠλίας ἐποίησε (as Elijah did), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when His disciples James and John saw this, they said, Lord, do You want us to command fire to come down from heaven and consume <mark class="chg">them, just as Elijah did?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when His disciples James and John saw this, they said, Lord, do You want us to command fire to come down from heaven and consume <mark class="chg">them?</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds the rebuke, &#x27;You do not know what manner of spirit you are of&#x27;" data-ref="Luke 9:55"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 9:55</span><span class="srnote">TR adds the rebuke, 'You do not know what manner of spirit you are of'</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But He turned and rebuked them, <mark class="chg">and said, You do not know what manner of spirit you are of.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But He turned and rebuked them,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds &#x27;the Son of Man did not come to destroy lives but to save them&#x27;" data-ref="Luke 9:56"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 9:56</span><span class="srnote">TR adds 'the Son of Man did not come to destroy lives but to save them'</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">For the Son of Man did not come to destroy men's lives but to save them.</mark> And they went to another village.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And they went to another village.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads the participle ὑψωθεῖσα, not the critical question" data-ref="Luke 10:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 10:15</span><span class="srnote">TR reads the participle ὑψωθεῖσα, not the critical question</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And you, Capernaum, <mark class="chg">who are</mark> exalted to <mark class="chg">heaven,</mark> will be brought down to Hades.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And you, Capernaum, <mark class="chg">will you be</mark> exalted to <mark class="chg">heaven? <tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἑνὸς δέ ἐστι χρεία (one thing is needed); 개역개정&#x27;s 몇 가지만 하든지 혹은 follows the critical text&#x27;s ὀλίγων δὲ ἐστιν χρεία ἤ" data-ref="Luke 10:42"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 10:42</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἑνὸς δέ ἐστι χρεία (one thing is needed); 개역개정's 몇 가지만 하든지 혹은 follows the critical text's ὀλίγων δὲ ἐστιν χρεία ἤ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But <mark class="chg">one thing is</mark> needed, <mark class="chg">and</mark> Mary has chosen that good part, which will not be taken away from her.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But <mark class="chg">few things are</mark> needed, <mark class="chg">or indeed only one.</mark> Mary has chosen that good part, which will not be taken away from her.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has Πάτερ ἡμῶν ὁ ἐν τοῖς οὐρανοῖς and the clause Γενηθήτω τὸ θέλημά σου, ὡς ἐν οὐρανῷ, καὶ ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς; both absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 11:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 11:2</span><span class="srnote">TR has Πάτερ ἡμῶν ὁ ἐν τοῖς οὐρανοῖς and the clause Γενηθήτω τὸ θέλημά σου, ὡς ἐν οὐρανῷ, καὶ ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς; both absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So He said to them, When you pray, say: <mark class="chg">Our Father in heaven,</mark> hallowed be Your name. <tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἀλλὰ ῥῦσαι ἡμᾶς ἀπὸ τοῦ πονηροῦ (but deliver us from the evil one)" data-ref="Luke 11:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 11:4</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἀλλὰ ῥῦσαι ἡμᾶς ἀπὸ τοῦ πονηροῦ (but deliver us from the evil one)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And forgive us our sins, for we also forgive everyone who is indebted to us. And do not lead us into <mark class="chg">temptation.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has the bread/stone clause (ἄρτον, μὴ λίθον ἐπιδώσει αὐτῷ), which 개역개정 dropped, keeping only the fish clause" data-ref="Luke 11:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 11:11</span><span class="srnote">TR has the bread/stone clause (ἄρτον, μὴ λίθον ἐπιδώσει αὐτῷ), which 개역개정 dropped, keeping only the fish clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If a son asks for <mark class="chg">bread</mark> from any of you who is a father, will he give him a <mark class="chg">stone? Or if he asks for a fish, will he give him a</mark> serpent instead of a fish?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If a son asks for <mark class="chg">fish</mark> from any of you who is a father, will he give him a serpent instead of a fish?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τοῦ προφήτου (the prophet) with Jonah; 개역개정 omits 선지자" data-ref="Luke 11:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 11:29</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τοῦ προφήτου (the prophet) with Jonah; 개역개정 omits 선지자</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when the multitudes were gathering together to Him, He began to say, This is an evil generation. It seeks a sign, and no sign will be given to it except the sign of <mark class="chg">Jonah the prophet.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when the multitudes were gathering together to Him, He began to say, This is an evil generation. It seeks a sign, and no sign will be given to it except the sign of <mark class="chg">Jonah.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR addresses γραμματεῖς καὶ Φαρισαῖοι, ὑποκριταί; 개역개정 has only the bare 너희여" data-ref="Luke 11:44"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 11:44</span><span class="srnote">TR addresses γραμματεῖς καὶ Φαρισαῖοι, ὑποκριταί; 개역개정 has only the bare 너희여</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Woe to <mark class="chg">you, scribes and Pharisees, hypocrites!</mark> For you are like graves which are not seen, and the men who walk over them are not aware of them.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Woe to <mark class="chg">you!</mark> For you are like graves which are not seen, and the men who walk over them are not aware of them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads Λέγοντος δὲ αὐτοῦ ταῦτα πρὸς αὐτούς; 개역개정&#x27;s 거기서 나오실 때에 follows the critical text&#x27;s κἀκεῖθεν ἐξελθόντος" data-ref="Luke 11:53"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 11:53</span><span class="srnote">TR reads Λέγοντος δὲ αὐτοῦ ταῦτα πρὸς αὐτούς; 개역개정's 거기서 나오실 때에 follows the critical text's κἀκεῖθεν ἐξελθόντος</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And as He <mark class="chg">said these things to them,</mark> the scribes and the Pharisees began to assail Him vehemently, and to cross-examine Him about many things,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And as He <mark class="chg">went out from there,</mark> the scribes and the Pharisees began to assail Him vehemently, and to cross-examine Him about many things,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τὴν βασιλείαν τοῦ Θεοῦ (the kingdom of God) and ταῦτα πάντα; 개역개정&#x27;s 그의 나라 follows the critical text&#x27;s αὐτοῦ" data-ref="Luke 12:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 12:31</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τὴν βασιλείαν τοῦ Θεοῦ (the kingdom of God) and ταῦτα πάντα; 개역개정's 그의 나라 follows the critical text's αὐτοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But seek <mark class="chg">the kingdom of God,</mark> and <mark class="chg">all</mark> these things will be added to you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But seek <mark class="chg">His kingdom,</mark> and these things will be added to you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἐγρηγόρησεν ἄν (he would have watched), missing in 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 12:39"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 12:39</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἐγρηγόρησεν ἄν (he would have watched), missing in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But know this, that if the master of the house had known what hour the thief would come, he would <mark class="chg">have watched and</mark> not allowed his house to be broken into.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But know this, that if the master of the house had known what hour the thief would come, he would not <mark class="chg">have</mark> allowed his house to be broken into.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὄνος ἢ βοῦς (a donkey or an ox); 개역개정&#x27;s 그 아들이나 소 follows the critical text&#x27;s υἱός" data-ref="Luke 14:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 14:5</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὄνος ἢ βοῦς (a donkey or an ox); 개역개정's 그 아들이나 소 follows the critical text's υἱός</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then He answered them, saying, Which of you, having a <mark class="chg">donkey</mark> or an ox that has fallen into a pit, will not immediately pull him out on the Sabbath day?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then He answered them, saying, Which of you, having a <mark class="chg">son</mark> or an ox that has fallen into a pit, will not immediately pull him out on the Sabbath day?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ends with Οὐ δοκῶ (I think not), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 17:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 17:9</span><span class="srnote">TR ends with Οὐ δοκῶ (I think not), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Does he thank that servant because he did the things that were commanded him? <mark class="chg">I think not.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Does he thank that servant because he did the things that were commanded him?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR" data-ref="Luke 17:36"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 17:36</span><span class="srnote">printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Two men will be in the field: the one will be taken and the other left.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads περίλυπον γενόμενον (saw that he became very sorrowful); 개역개정 follows the critical text&#x27;s bare Ἰδὼν δὲ αὐτὸν" data-ref="Luke 18:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 18:24</span><span class="srnote">TR reads περίλυπον γενόμενον (saw that he became very sorrowful); 개역개정 follows the critical text's bare Ἰδὼν δὲ αὐτὸν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when Jesus saw <mark class="chg">that he became very sorrowful,</mark> He said, How hard it is for those who have riches to enter the kingdom of God!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when Jesus saw <mark class="chg">him,</mark> He said, How hard it is for those who have riches to enter the kingdom of God!</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has τοὺς πωλοῦντας ἐν αὐτῷ καὶ ἀγοράζοντας (those selling and buying); 개역개정 keeps only the sellers" data-ref="Luke 19:45"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 19:45</span><span class="srnote">TR has τοὺς πωλοῦντας ἐν αὐτῷ καὶ ἀγοράζοντας (those selling and buying); 개역개정 keeps only the sellers</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then He went into the temple and began to drive out those who were <mark class="chg">buying and</mark> selling in it,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then He went into the temple and began to drive out those who were selling in it,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἴσως τοῦτον ἰδόντες ἐντραπήσονται (perhaps when they see him); 개역개정 drops ἰδόντες" data-ref="Luke 20:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 20:13</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἴσως τοῦτον ἰδόντες ἐντραπήσονται (perhaps when they see him); 개역개정 drops ἰδόντες</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then the owner of the vineyard said, What shall I do? I will send my beloved son. Probably they will respect <mark class="chg">him when they see</mark> him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then the owner of the vineyard said, What shall I do? I will send my beloved son. Probably they will respect him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR includes the question Τί με πειράζετε; (why do you test me); 개역개정 breaks off at 이르시되" data-ref="Luke 20:23"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 20:23</span><span class="srnote">TR includes the question Τί με πειράζετε; (why do you test me); 개역개정 breaks off at 이르시되</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But He perceived their craftiness, and said to them, <mark class="chg">Why do you test Me?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But He perceived their craftiness, and said to them,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR: the second took her AND died childless" data-ref="Luke 20:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 20:30</span><span class="srnote">TR: the second took her AND died childless</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And the second took her as <mark class="chg">wife, and he died childless.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And the second <mark class="chg">and the third</mark> took her as <mark class="chg">wife.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens the verse with the third taking her" data-ref="Luke 20:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 20:31</span><span class="srnote">TR opens the verse with the third taking her</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And the <mark class="chg">third took her as wife; and</mark> in like <mark class="chg">manner the seven also</mark> left no <mark class="chg">children,</mark> and died.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And the <mark class="chg">seven also,</mark> in like <mark class="chg">manner,</mark> left no <mark class="chg">children</mark> and died.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads εἰς τὰ δῶρα τοῦ Θεοῦ (the gifts of God); 개역개정 drops the genitive τοῦ Θεοῦ" data-ref="Luke 21:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 21:4</span><span class="srnote">TR reads εἰς τὰ δῶρα τοῦ Θεοῦ (the gifts of God); 개역개정 drops the genitive τοῦ Θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for all these out of their abundance have put in <mark class="chg">offerings for God,</mark> but she out of her poverty has put in all the livelihood that she had.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for all these out of their abundance have put in <mark class="chg">offerings,</mark> but she out of her poverty has put in all the livelihood that she had.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR καταξιωθῆτε (&#x27;be counted worthy&#x27;), not the critical κατισχύσητε" data-ref="Luke 21:36"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 21:36</span><span class="srnote">TR καταξιωθῆτε ('be counted worthy'), not the critical κατισχύσητε</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Watch therefore, and pray always that you may be <mark class="chg">counted worthy</mark> to escape all these things that will come to pass, and to stand before the Son of Man.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Watch therefore, and pray always that you may be <mark class="chg">able</mark> to escape all these things that will come to pass, and to stand before the Son of Man.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads οἱ δώδεκα ἀπόστολοι (the twelve apostles); 개역개정 drops the number 열둘" data-ref="Luke 22:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 22:14</span><span class="srnote">TR reads οἱ δώδεκα ἀπόστολοι (the twelve apostles); 개역개정 drops the number 열둘</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">When the hour had come, He sat down, and the <mark class="chg">twelve</mark> apostles with Him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">When the hour had come, He sat down, and the apostles with Him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens with Εἶπε δὲ ὁ Κύριος (and the Lord said); 개역개정 omits the speech introduction" data-ref="Luke 22:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 22:31</span><span class="srnote">TR opens with Εἶπε δὲ ὁ Κύριος (and the Lord said); 개역개정 omits the speech introduction</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">And the Lord said,</mark> Simon, Simon! Indeed, Satan has asked for you, that he may sift you as wheat.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Simon, Simon! Indeed, Satan has asked for you, that he may sift you as wheat.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἐτυπτον αὐτοῦ τὸ πρόσωπον καί (they struck his face and), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 22:64"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 22:64</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἐτυπτον αὐτοῦ τὸ πρόσωπον καί (they struck his face and), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And having blindfolded Him, they <mark class="chg">struck Him on the face and</mark> asked Him, saying, Prophesy! Who is the one who struck You?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And having blindfolded Him, they asked Him, saying, Prophesy! Who is the one who struck You?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds μοι ἢ ἀπολύσητε (me, or let me go); 개역개정 kept only 대답하지 아니할 것이니라" data-ref="Luke 22:68"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 22:68</span><span class="srnote">TR adds μοι ἢ ἀπολύσητε (me, or let me go); 개역개정 kept only 대답하지 아니할 것이니라</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But if I also ask you, you will not answer <mark class="chg">Me, nor let Me go.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But if I also ask you, you will not answer <mark class="chg">Me.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἀνέπεμψα ὑμᾶς (&#x27;I sent you back&#x27;), not ἀνέπεμψεν αὐτόν" data-ref="Luke 23:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 23:15</span><span class="srnote">TR ἀνέπεμψα ὑμᾶς ('I sent you back'), not ἀνέπεμψεν αὐτόν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">No, neither did Herod, for <mark class="chg">I</mark> sent <mark class="chg">you</mark> back to <mark class="chg">him;</mark> and indeed nothing deserving of death has been done by Him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">No, neither did Herod, for <mark class="chg">he</mark> sent <mark class="chg">Him</mark> back to <mark class="chg">us;</mark> and indeed nothing deserving of death has been done by Him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR" data-ref="Luke 23:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 23:17</span><span class="srnote">printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">for it was necessary for him to release one to them at the feast.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR αἱ φωναὶ αὐτῶν καὶ τῶν ἀρχιερέων (their voices and of the chief priests); 개역개정 omits 대제사장들" data-ref="Luke 23:23"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 23:23</span><span class="srnote">TR αἱ φωναὶ αὐτῶν καὶ τῶν ἀρχιερέων (their voices and of the chief priests); 개역개정 omits 대제사장들</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But they were insistent, demanding with loud voices that He be crucified. And their voices <mark class="chg">and those of the chief priests</mark> prevailed.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But they were insistent, demanding with loud voices that He be crucified. And their voices prevailed.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐπιγραφὴ γεγραμμένη ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ γράμμασιν Ἑλληνικοῖς καὶ Ῥωμαϊκοῖς καὶ Ἑβραϊκοῖς (in Greek, Latin and Hebrew letters); 개역개정 dropped the three scripts" data-ref="Luke 23:38"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 23:38</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐπιγραφὴ γεγραμμένη ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ γράμμασιν Ἑλληνικοῖς καὶ Ῥωμαϊκοῖς καὶ Ἑβραϊκοῖς (in Greek, Latin and Hebrew letters); 개역개정 dropped the three scripts</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And an inscription also was written over <mark class="chg">Him in letters of Greek, Latin, and Hebrew:</mark> THIS IS THE KING OF THE JEWS.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And an inscription also was written over <mark class="chg">Him:</mark> THIS IS THE KING OF THE JEWS.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καί τινες σὺν αὐταῖς (and certain others with them), dropped in 개역개정" data-ref="Luke 24:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 24:1</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καί τινες σὺν αὐταῖς (and certain others with them), dropped in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now on the first day of the week, very early in the morning, <mark class="chg">they, and certain other women with them,</mark> came to the tomb bringing the spices which they had prepared.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now on the first day of the week, very early in the morning, <mark class="chg">they</mark> came to the tomb bringing the spices which they had prepared.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καὶ ἀπὸ μελισσίου κηρίου (and some honeycomb); NKJV also has it" data-ref="Luke 24:42"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 24:42</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καὶ ἀπὸ μελισσίου κηρίου (and some honeycomb); NKJV also has it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So they gave Him a piece of a broiled <mark class="chg">fish and some honeycomb.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So they gave Him a piece of a broiled <mark class="chg">fish.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads αἰνοῦντες καὶ εὐλογοῦντες τὸν θεόν and closes the Gospel with Ἀμήν; 개역개정 has 찬송 only and no 아멘" data-ref="Luke 24:53"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Luke 24:53</span><span class="srnote">TR reads αἰνοῦντες καὶ εὐλογοῦντες τὸν θεόν and closes the Gospel with Ἀμήν; 개역개정 has 찬송 only and no 아멘</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and were continually in the temple praising <mark class="chg">and blessing</mark> God. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and were continually in the temple praising God.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">John</span><span class="bkcount">35</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ὁ μονογενὴς υἱός (only begotten Son); 개역개정 follows the critical text θεός (독생하신 하나님)" data-ref="John 1:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 1:18</span><span class="srnote">TR ὁ μονογενὴς υἱός (only begotten Son); 개역개정 follows the critical text θεός (독생하신 하나님)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">No one has seen God at any time. The only begotten <mark class="chg">Son,</mark> who is in the bosom of the Father, He has declared Him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">No one has seen God at any time. The only begotten <mark class="chg">God</mark> who is in the bosom of the Father, He has declared Him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὃς ἔμπροσθεν μου γέγονεν (who is preferred before me); 개역개정 has only 내 뒤에 오시는 그이라" data-ref="John 1:27"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 1:27</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὃς ἔμπροσθεν μου γέγονεν (who is preferred before me); 개역개정 has only 내 뒤에 오시는 그이라</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">It is He who comes after <mark class="chg">me, who is preferred before</mark> me, whose sandal strap I am not worthy to loose.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">It is He who comes after me, whose sandal strap I am not worthy to loose.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Βηθαβαρᾶ, critical Βηθανία; Korean 베다니 is the critical name" data-ref="John 1:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 1:28</span><span class="srnote">TR Βηθαβαρᾶ, critical Βηθανία; Korean 베다니 is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">These things were done in <mark class="chg">Bethabara</mark> beyond the Jordan, where John was baptizing.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">These things were done in <mark class="chg">Bethany</mark> beyond the Jordan, where John was baptizing.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); Korean 요한 is critical" data-ref="John 1:42"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 1:42</span><span class="srnote">TR Ἰωνᾶ (Jonah), critical Ἰωάννου (John); Korean 요한 is critical</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And he brought him to Jesus. Now when Jesus looked at him, He said, You are Simon the son of <mark class="chg">Jonah.</mark> You shall be called Cephas (which is translated, A Stone).</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And he brought him to Jesus. Now when Jesus looked at him, He said, You are Simon the son of <mark class="chg">John.</mark> You shall be called Cephas (which is translated, A Stone).</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἀπ᾽ ἄρτι (hereafter / from now on), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="John 1:51"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 1:51</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἀπ᾽ ἄρτι (hereafter / from now on), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And He said to him, Most assuredly, I say to you, <mark class="chg">hereafter</mark> you shall see heaven open, and the angels of God ascending and descending upon the Son of Man.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And He said to him, Most assuredly, I say to you, you shall see heaven open, and the angels of God ascending and descending upon the Son of Man.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὁ ὢν ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ (who is in heaven); 개역개정 omits the clause" data-ref="John 3:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">John 3:13</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὁ ὢν ἐν τῷ οὐρανῷ (who is in heaven); 개역개정 omits the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">No one has ascended to heaven but He who came down from heaven, that is, the Son of <mark class="chg">Man who is in heaven.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">No one has ascended to heaven but He who came down from heaven, that is, the Son of <mark class="chg">Man.</mark></span></span></tr>
