<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens with Ἀδελφοί (형제들아), a vocative 개역개정 drops" data-ref="Acts 20:32"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 20:32</span><span class="srnote">TR opens with Ἀδελφοί (형제들아), a vocative 개역개정 drops</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So <mark class="chg">now, brethren,</mark> I commend you to God and to the word of His grace, which is able to build you up and give you an inheritance among all those who are sanctified.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So <mark class="chg">now</mark> I commend you to God and to the word of His grace, which is able to build you up and give you an inheritance among all those who are sanctified.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐδόξαζον τὸν Κύριον (glorified the Lord), not τὸν θεόν (하나님)" data-ref="Acts 21:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 21:20</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐδόξαζον τὸν Κύριον (glorified the Lord), not τὸν θεόν (하나님)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when they heard it, they glorified <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark> And they said to him, You see, brother, how many myriads of Jews there are who have believed, and they are all zealous for the law.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when they heard it, they glorified <mark class="chg">God.</mark> And they said to him, You see, brother, how many myriads of Jews there are who have believed, and they are all zealous for the law.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR δεῖ πλῆθος συνελθεῖν (the multitude must assemble) is missing from 개역개정" data-ref="Acts 21:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 21:22</span><span class="srnote">TR δεῖ πλῆθος συνελθεῖν (the multitude must assemble) is missing from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">What then? <mark class="chg">The assembly must certainly meet, for they</mark> will hear that you have come.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">What then? <mark class="chg">They</mark> will <mark class="chg">certainly</mark> hear that you have come.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR μηδὲν τοιοῦτον τηρεῖν αὐτούς, εἰ μὴ (that they observe nothing of the kind except) is absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Acts 21:25"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 21:25</span><span class="srnote">TR μηδὲν τοιοῦτον τηρεῖν αὐτούς, εἰ μὴ (that they observe nothing of the kind except) is absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But concerning the Gentiles who believe, we have written and decided that they should <mark class="chg">observe nothing of the kind, except that they</mark> keep themselves from things offered to idols, from blood, from things strangled, and from sexual immorality.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But concerning the Gentiles who believe, we have written and decided that they should keep themselves from things offered to idols, from blood, from things strangled, and from sexual immorality.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR καὶ ἔμφοβοι ἐγένοντο (and were afraid); 개역개정 omits 두려워하였으나" data-ref="Acts 22:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 22:9</span><span class="srnote">TR καὶ ἔμφοβοι ἐγένοντο (and were afraid); 개역개정 omits 두려워하였으나</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And those who were with me indeed saw the <mark class="chg">light and were afraid,</mark> but they did not hear the voice of Him who spoke to me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And those who were with me indeed saw the <mark class="chg">light,</mark> but they did not hear the voice of Him who spoke to me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads μὴ θεομαχῶμεν (let us not fight against God); 개역개정 follows the critical text&#x27;s open question 어찌 하겠느냐" data-ref="Acts 23:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 23:9</span><span class="srnote">TR reads μὴ θεομαχῶμεν (let us not fight against God); 개역개정 follows the critical text's open question 어찌 하겠느냐</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then there arose a loud outcry. And the scribes of the Pharisees' party arose and protested, saying, We find no evil in this man; but if a spirit or an angel has spoken to him, <mark class="chg">let us not fight against God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then there arose a loud outcry. And the scribes of the Pharisees' party arose and protested, saying, We find no evil in this man; but if a spirit or an angel has spoken to him, <mark class="chg">what then?</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has the vocative Παῦλε (Θάρσει Παῦλε); 개역개정 drops the name" data-ref="Acts 23:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 23:11</span><span class="srnote">TR has the vocative Παῦλε (Θάρσει Παῦλε); 개역개정 drops the name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But the following night the Lord stood by him and said, Be of good <mark class="chg">cheer, Paul;</mark> for as you have testified for Me in Jerusalem, so you must also bear witness at Rome.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But the following night the Lord stood by him and said, Be of good <mark class="chg">cheer;</mark> for as you have testified for Me in Jerusalem, so you must also bear witness at Rome.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the letter with Ἔρρωσο (Farewell), which 개역개정 omits; the impersonal passive already carries ὑπὸ τῶν Ἰουδαίων" data-ref="Acts 23:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 23:30</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the letter with Ἔρρωσο (Farewell), which 개역개정 omits; the impersonal passive already carries ὑπὸ τῶν Ἰουδαίων</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And when it was told me that the Jews lay in wait for the man, I sent him to you immediately, and also commanded his accusers to state before you the charges against him. <mark class="chg">Farewell.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And when it was told me that the Jews lay in wait for the man, I sent him to you immediately, and also commanded his accusers to state before you the charges against him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καὶ κατὰ τὸν ἡμέτερον νόμον ἠθελήσαμεν κρίνειν (we wanted to judge him by our law), absent from the critical text" data-ref="Acts 24:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:6</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καὶ κατὰ τὸν ἡμέτερον νόμον ἠθελήσαμεν κρίνειν (we wanted to judge him by our law), absent from the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He also tried to profane the temple, and we seized <mark class="chg">him, and wanted to judge him according to our law.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He also tried to profane the temple, and we seized <mark class="chg">him.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="개역개정 has no verse 7 here; the TR does" data-ref="Acts 24:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:7</span><span class="srnote">개역개정 has no verse 7 here; the TR does</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="rest">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">But the commander Lysias came by and with great violence took him out of our hands.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens with κελεύσας τοὺς κατηγόρους αὐτοῦ ἔρχεσθαι ἐπὶ σέ; 개역개정 follows the critical text, which lacks the clause" data-ref="Acts 24:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:8</span><span class="srnote">TR opens with κελεύσας τοὺς κατηγόρους αὐτοῦ ἔρχεσθαι ἐπὶ σέ; 개역개정 follows the critical text, which lacks the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">commanding his accusers to come to you.</mark> By examining him yourself you may ascertain all these things of which we accuse him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">By examining him yourself you may ascertain all these things of which we accuse him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὅπως λύσῃ αὐτόν (that he might release him), the motive behind the bribes" data-ref="Acts 24:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 24:26</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὅπως λύσῃ αὐτόν (that he might release him), the motive behind the bribes</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Meanwhile he also hoped that money would be given him by <mark class="chg">Paul, that he might release him.</mark> Therefore he sent for him more often and conversed with him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Meanwhile he also hoped that money would be given him by <mark class="chg">Paul.</mark> Therefore he sent for him more often and conversed with him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has εἰς ἀπώλειαν (to destruction); 개역개정&#x27;s 내주는 것 alone loses it" data-ref="Acts 25:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 25:16</span><span class="srnote">TR has εἰς ἀπώλειαν (to destruction); 개역개정's 내주는 것 alone loses it</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To whom I answered, It is not the custom of the Romans to deliver up any man <mark class="chg">to destruction</mark> before the accused has met the accusers face to face, and has had opportunity to answer for himself concerning the charge.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To whom I answered, It is not the custom of the Romans to deliver up any man before the accused has met the accusers face to face, and has had opportunity to answer for himself concerning the charge.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens Καὶ ταῦτα εἰπόντος αὐτοῦ (when he had said these things), dropped in 개역개정" data-ref="Acts 26:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 26:30</span><span class="srnote">TR opens Καὶ ταῦτα εἰπόντος αὐτοῦ (when he had said these things), dropped in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">And when he had said these things, the</mark> king, the governor, Bernice, and those who sat with them all rose up.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The</mark> king, the governor, Bernice, and those who sat with them all rose up.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐρρίψαμεν (we threw) with αὐτόχειρες; 개역개정 follows the critical text&#x27;s ἐρρίψαν (they)" data-ref="Acts 27:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 27:19</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐρρίψαμεν (we threw) with αὐτόχειρες; 개역개정 follows the critical text's ἐρρίψαν (they)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">On the third day <mark class="chg">we</mark> threw the ship's tackle overboard with <mark class="chg">our</mark> own hands.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">On the third day <mark class="chg">they</mark> threw the ship's tackle overboard with <mark class="chg">their</mark> own hands.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ὁ ἑκατόνταρχος παρέδωκε τοὺς δεσμίους τῷ στρατοπεδάρχῃ; 친위대장 renders στρατοπεδάρχης (cf. 새번역)" data-ref="Acts 28:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 28:16</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ὁ ἑκατόνταρχος παρέδωκε τοὺς δεσμίους τῷ στρατοπεδάρχῃ; 친위대장 renders στρατοπεδάρχης (cf. 새번역)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now when we came to Rome, <mark class="chg">the centurion delivered the prisoners to the captain of the guard; but</mark> Paul was permitted to dwell by himself with the soldier who guarded him.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now when we came to Rome, Paul was permitted to dwell by himself with the soldier who guarded him.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR" data-ref="Acts 28:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Acts 28:29</span><span class="srnote">printed (없음) in 개역개정; present in the TR</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">And when he had said these words, the Jews departed and had a great dispute among themselves.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Romans</span><span class="bkcount">27</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has 복음 alone" data-ref="Romans 1:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 1:16</span><span class="srnote">TR τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has 복음 alone</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For I am not ashamed of the <mark class="chg">gospel of Christ,</mark> for it is the power of God to salvation for everyone who believes, for the Jew first and also for the Greek.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I am not ashamed of the <mark class="chg">gospel,</mark> for it is the power of God to salvation for everyone who believes, for the Jew first and also for the Greek.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR list includes πορνείᾳ between ἀδικίᾳ and πονηρίᾳ; 개역개정 omits 음행" data-ref="Romans 1:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 1:29</span><span class="srnote">TR list includes πορνείᾳ between ἀδικίᾳ and πονηρίᾳ; 개역개정 omits 음행</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">being filled with all unrighteousness, <mark class="chg">sexual immorality,</mark> wickedness, covetousness, maliciousness; full of envy, murder, strife, deceit, evil-mindedness; they are whisperers,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">being filled with all unrighteousness, wickedness, covetousness, maliciousness; full of envy, murder, strife, deceit, evil-mindedness; they are whisperers,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἀσπόνδους (&#x27;implacable&#x27;) to the vice list" data-ref="Romans 1:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 1:31</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἀσπόνδους ('implacable') to the vice list</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">without understanding, untrustworthy, unloving, <mark class="chg">unforgiving,</mark> unmerciful;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">without understanding, untrustworthy, unloving, unmerciful;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR οὐ κατενόησεν (he did not consider his own body dead); 개역개정&#x27;s 알고도 follows the critical text&#x27;s κατενόησεν" data-ref="Romans 4:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 4:19</span><span class="srnote">TR οὐ κατενόησεν (he did not consider his own body dead); 개역개정's 알고도 follows the critical text's κατενόησεν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And being about a hundred years old, <mark class="chg">not considering</mark> his own body already dead and the deadness of Sarah's womb, he did not become weak in faith,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And being about a hundred years old, <mark class="chg">knowing that</mark> his own body <mark class="chg">was</mark> already dead and the deadness of Sarah's womb, he did not become weak in faith,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds μὴ κατὰ σάρκα περιπατοῦσιν ἀλλὰ κατὰ πνεῦμα" data-ref="Romans 8:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 8:1</span><span class="srnote">TR adds μὴ κατὰ σάρκα περιπατοῦσιν ἀλλὰ κατὰ πνεῦμα</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">There is therefore now no condemnation to those who are in Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus, who do not walk according to the flesh, but according to the Spirit.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">There is therefore now no condemnation to those who are in Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ηλευθερωσε με (set me free); 개역개정 has 너를, following the critical ηλευθερωσεν σε" data-ref="Romans 8:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 8:2</span><span class="srnote">TR ηλευθερωσε με (set me free); 개역개정 has 너를, following the critical ηλευθερωσεν σε</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For the law of the Spirit of life in Christ Jesus has made <mark class="chg">me</mark> free from the law of sin and death.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For the law of the Spirit of life in Christ Jesus has made <mark class="chg">you</mark> free from the law of sin and death.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has λογον γαρ συντελων και συντεμνων εν δικαιοσυνη before the οτι clause; 개역개정 omits the whole first half of the Isaiah 10:22-23 quotation" data-ref="Romans 9:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 9:28</span><span class="srnote">TR has λογον γαρ συντελων και συντεμνων εν δικαιοσυνη before the οτι clause; 개역개정 omits the whole first half of the Isaiah 10:22-23 quotation</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For <mark class="chg">He will finish the work and cut it short in righteousness, because</mark> the Lord will make a short work upon the earth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For the Lord will make a short work upon the earth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR repeats νομον δικαιοσυνης (law of righteousness); 개역개정 has 율법 only for the second occurrence" data-ref="Romans 9:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 9:31</span><span class="srnote">TR repeats νομον δικαιοσυνης (law of righteousness); 개역개정 has 율법 only for the second occurrence</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but Israel, pursuing the law of righteousness, has not attained to <mark class="chg">the law of righteousness.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but Israel, pursuing the law of righteousness, has not attained to <mark class="chg">that law.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ως εξ εργων νομου (works of the law); 개역개정 has 행위 only" data-ref="Romans 9:32"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 9:32</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ως εξ εργων νομου (works of the law); 개역개정 has 행위 only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Why? Because they did not seek it by faith, but as it were, by <mark class="chg">the works of the law.</mark> They stumbled at that stumbling stone.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Why? Because they did not seek it by faith, but as it were, by <mark class="chg">works.</mark> They stumbled at that stumbling stone.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ειρηνην των ευαγγελιζομενων (who preach the gospel of peace), dropped by 개역개정" data-ref="Romans 10:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 10:15</span><span class="srnote">TR has ειρηνην των ευαγγελιζομενων (who preach the gospel of peace), dropped by 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And how shall they preach unless they are sent? As it is written: How beautiful are the feet of those who preach the <mark class="chg">gospel of peace!</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And how shall they preach unless they are sent? As it is written: How beautiful are the feet of those who preach the <mark class="chg">good news!</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ρηματος Θεου (word of God), not Χριστου (그리스도의) as in 개역개정 from the critical text" data-ref="Romans 10:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 10:17</span><span class="srnote">TR ρηματος Θεου (word of God), not Χριστου (그리스도의) as in 개역개정 from the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">So then faith comes by hearing, and hearing by the word of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">So then faith comes by hearing, and hearing by the word of <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR carries the second, parallel half (ει δε εξ εργων, ουκετι εστι χαρις, επει το εργον ουκετι εστιν εργον), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Romans 11:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 11:6</span><span class="srnote">TR carries the second, parallel half (ει δε εξ εργων, ουκετι εστι χαρις, επει το εργον ουκετι εστιν εργον), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And if by grace, then it is no longer of works; otherwise grace is no longer grace. <mark class="chg">But if it is of works, it is no longer grace; otherwise work is no longer work.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And if by grace, then it is no longer of works; otherwise grace is no longer grace.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ου ψευδομαρτυρησεις (you shall not bear false witness) between the theft and coveting commands; 개역개정 drops it with the critical text" data-ref="Romans 13:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 13:9</span><span class="srnote">TR has ου ψευδομαρτυρησεις (you shall not bear false witness) between the theft and coveting commands; 개역개정 drops it with the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For the commandments, You shall not commit adultery, You shall not murder, You shall not steal, <mark class="chg">You shall not bear false witness,</mark> You shall not covet, and if there is any other commandment, are all summed up in this saying, namely, You shall love your neighbor as yourself.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For the commandments, You shall not commit adultery, You shall not murder, You shall not steal, You shall not covet, and if there is any other commandment, are all summed up in this saying, namely, You shall love your neighbor as yourself.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads δυνατος γαρ εστιν ο Θεος (God is able) rather than δυνατει ο κυριος; 개역개정 has 주께, following the critical text" data-ref="Romans 14:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 14:4</span><span class="srnote">TR reads δυνατος γαρ εστιν ο Θεος (God is able) rather than δυνατει ο κυριος; 개역개정 has 주께, following the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Who are you to judge another's servant? To his own master he stands or falls. Indeed, he will be made to stand, for <mark class="chg">God</mark> is able to make him stand.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Who are you to judge another's servant? To his own master he stands or falls. Indeed, he will be made to stand, for <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> is able to make him stand.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds και ο μη φρονων την ημεραν, Κυριω ου φρονει (he who does not regard the day, to the Lord he does not regard it), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Romans 14:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 14:6</span><span class="srnote">TR adds και ο μη φρονων την ημεραν, Κυριω ου φρονει (he who does not regard the day, to the Lord he does not regard it), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He who observes the day, observes it to the <mark class="chg">Lord; and he who does not observe the day, to the Lord he does not observe it.</mark> He who eats, eats to the Lord, for he gives God thanks; and he who does not eat, to the Lord he does not eat, and gives God thanks.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He who observes the day, observes it to the <mark class="chg">Lord.</mark> He who eats, eats to the Lord, for he gives God thanks; and he who does not eat, to the Lord he does not eat, and gives God thanks.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR βηματος του Χριστου (judgment seat of Christ); 개역개정 has 하나님의, following the critical του Θεου" data-ref="Romans 14:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 14:10</span><span class="srnote">TR βηματος του Χριστου (judgment seat of Christ); 개역개정 has 하나님의, following the critical του Θεου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But why do you judge your brother? Or why do you show contempt for your brother? For we shall all stand before the judgment seat of <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But why do you judge your brother? Or why do you show contempt for your brother? For we shall all stand before the judgment seat of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads Ιησουν Χριστον (Jesus Christ); 개역개정 has 그리스도 only" data-ref="Romans 15:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:8</span><span class="srnote">TR reads Ιησουν Χριστον (Jesus Christ); 개역개정 has 그리스도 only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now I say that <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> Christ has become a servant to the circumcision for the truth of God, to confirm the promises made to the fathers,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now I say that Christ has become a servant to the circumcision for the truth of God, to confirm the promises made to the fathers,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens with the vocative αδελφοι (형제들아), which 개역개정 drops" data-ref="Romans 15:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:15</span><span class="srnote">TR opens with the vocative αδελφοι (형제들아), which 개역개정 drops</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Nevertheless, brethren,</mark> I have written more boldly to you on some points, as reminding you, because of the grace given to me by God,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Nevertheless</mark> I have written more boldly to you on some points, as reminding you, because of the grace given to me by God,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads εν δυναμει πνευματος Θεου (the Spirit of God); 개역개정 has 성령의 능력으로 only" data-ref="Romans 15:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:19</span><span class="srnote">TR reads εν δυναμει πνευματος Θεου (the Spirit of God); 개역개정 has 성령의 능력으로 only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">in mighty signs and wonders, by the power of the <mark class="chg">Spirit of God,</mark> so that from Jerusalem and round about to Illyricum I have fully preached the gospel of Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">in mighty signs and wonders, by the power of the <mark class="chg">Spirit,</mark> so that from Jerusalem and round about to Illyricum I have fully preached the gospel of Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ελευσομαι προς υμας (I will come to you) in this verse; the critical text carries the Spain clause in 15:23 instead, so 개역개정 leaves this verse without its main verb" data-ref="Romans 15:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:24</span><span class="srnote">TR has ελευσομαι προς υμας (I will come to you) in this verse; the critical text carries the Spain clause in 15:23 instead, so 개역개정 leaves this verse without its main verb</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Whenever I journey to Spain, I shall come to you.</mark> For I hope to see you as I pass by, and to be helped on my way there by you, if first I may enjoy your company for a while.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I hope to see you as I pass by, and to be helped on my way there by you, if first I may enjoy your company for a while.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ευλογιας του ευαγγελιου του Χριστου (blessing of the gospel of Christ); 개역개정 omits 복음의" data-ref="Romans 15:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 15:29</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ευλογιας του ευαγγελιου του Χριστου (blessing of the gospel of Christ); 개역개정 omits 복음의</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But I know that when I come to you, I shall come in the fullness of the blessing of <mark class="chg">the gospel of</mark> Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But I know that when I come to you, I shall come in the fullness of the blessing of Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Πρίσκιλλαν, critical Πρίσκαν; Korean 브리스가 is the critical name" data-ref="Romans 16:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:3</span><span class="srnote">TR Πρίσκιλλαν, critical Πρίσκαν; Korean 브리스가 is the critical name</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet <mark class="chg">Priscilla</mark> and Aquila, my fellow workers in Christ Jesus,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet <mark class="chg">Prisca</mark> and Aquila, my fellow workers in Christ Jesus,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads Αχαιας (Achaia); 개역개정 has 아시아, following the critical Ασιας" data-ref="Romans 16:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:5</span><span class="srnote">TR reads Αχαιας (Achaia); 개역개정 has 아시아, following the critical Ασιας</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet the church that is in their house. Greet my beloved Epaenetus, who is the firstfruits of <mark class="chg">Achaia</mark> to Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet the church that is in their house. Greet my beloved Epaenetus, who is the firstfruits of <mark class="chg">Asia</mark> to Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads εις ημας (for us), not εις υμας (너희를 위하여) as in 개역개정" data-ref="Romans 16:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:6</span><span class="srnote">TR reads εις ημας (for us), not εις υμας (너희를 위하여) as in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Greet Mary, who labored much for <mark class="chg">us.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Greet Mary, who labored much for <mark class="chg">you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τω Κυριω ημων Ιησου Χριστω; 개역개정 drops 예수" data-ref="Romans 16:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Romans 16:18</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τω Κυριω ημων Ιησου Χριστω; 개역개정 drops 예수</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For those who are such do not serve our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> Christ, but their own belly, and by smooth words and flattering speech deceive the hearts of the simple.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For those who are such do not serve our Lord Christ, but their own belly, and by smooth words and flattering speech deceive the hearts of the simple.</span></span></tr>

<tr class="tr" data-note="TR lists three things, ζηλος και ερις και διχοστασιαι (envy, strife, divisions); 개역개정 has 시기와 분쟁 only" data-ref="1 Corinthians 3:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 3:3</span><span class="srnote">TR lists three things, ζηλος και ερις και διχοστασιαι (envy, strife, divisions); 개역개정 has 시기와 분쟁 only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for you are still carnal. For where there <mark class="chg">is envy</mark> and <mark class="chg">strife</mark> among you, are you not carnal and behaving like mere men?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου in both places; 개역개정 drops 그리스도 twice" data-ref="1 Corinthians 5:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 5:4</span><span class="srnote">TR reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου in both places; 개역개정 drops 그리스도 twice</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">In the name of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> when you are gathered together, along with my spirit, with the power of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">In the name of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> when you are gathered together, along with my spirit, with the power of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads υπερ ημων εθυθη (was sacrificed for us); 개역개정 omits 우리를 위하여" data-ref="1 Corinthians 5:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 5:7</span><span class="srnote">TR reads υπερ ημων εθυθη (was sacrificed for us); 개역개정 omits 우리를 위하여</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore purge out the old leaven, that you may be a new lump, since you truly are unleavened. <tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds και εν τω πνευματι υμων, ατινα εστι του Θεου (and in your spirit, which are God&#x27;s), dropped by 개역개정" data-ref="1 Corinthians 6:20"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 6:20</span><span class="srnote">TR adds και εν τω πνευματι υμων, ατινα εστι του Θεου (and in your spirit, which are God's), dropped by 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For you were bought at a price; therefore glorify God in your <mark class="chg">body and in your spirit, which are God's.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For you were bought at a price; therefore glorify God in your <mark class="chg">body.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads την οφειλομενην ευνοιαν (the affection due); 개역개정 has 의무, following the critical οφειλην" data-ref="1 Corinthians 7:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 7:3</span><span class="srnote">TR reads την οφειλομενην ευνοιαν (the affection due); 개역개정 has 의무, following the critical οφειλην</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let the husband render to his wife the <mark class="chg">affection due</mark> her, and likewise also the wife to her husband.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let the husband render to his wife the <mark class="chg">duty owed</mark> her, and likewise also the wife to her husband.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τη νηστεια και τη προσευχη (fasting and prayer); 개역개정 has 기도 only, following the critical text" data-ref="1 Corinthians 7:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 7:5</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τη νηστεια και τη προσευχη (fasting and prayer); 개역개정 has 기도 only, following the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Do not deprive one another except with consent for a time, that you may give yourselves to <mark class="chg">fasting and</mark> prayer; and come together again so that Satan does not tempt you because of your lack of self-control.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Do not deprive one another except with consent for a time, that you may give yourselves to prayer; and come together again so that Satan does not tempt you because of your lack of self-control.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ως εμερισεν ο Θεος … ως κεκληκεν ο Κυριος; 개역개정 swaps the two subjects (주께서 나눠 주신 / 하나님이 부르신)" data-ref="1 Corinthians 7:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 7:17</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ως εμερισεν ο Θεος … ως κεκληκεν ο Κυριος; 개역개정 swaps the two subjects (주께서 나눠 주신 / 하나님이 부르신)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But as <mark class="chg">God</mark> has distributed to each one, as <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> has called each one, so let him walk.
