<tr class="tr" data-note="TR συνειδήσει (&#x27;conscience&#x27;), not the critical συνηθείᾳ (&#x27;habit&#x27;)" data-ref="1 Corinthians 8:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 8:7</span><span class="srnote">TR συνειδήσει ('conscience'), not the critical συνηθείᾳ ('habit')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">However, there is not in everyone that knowledge; for some, <mark class="chg">with consciousness</mark> of the <mark class="chg">idol,</mark> until <mark class="chg">now</mark> eat it as a thing offered to an idol; and their conscience, being weak, is defiled.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">However, there is not in everyone that knowledge; for some, <mark class="chg">from habit</mark> of the <mark class="chg">idol</mark> until <mark class="chg">now,</mark> eat it as a thing offered to an idol; and their conscience, being weak, is defiled.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the gospel of Christ); 개역개정 has only 복음" data-ref="1 Corinthians 9:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 9:18</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τὸ εὐαγγέλιον τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the gospel of Christ); 개역개정 has only 복음</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">What is my reward then? That when I preach the <mark class="chg">gospel of Christ,</mark> I may present the gospel without charge, that I may not abuse my authority in the gospel.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">What is my reward then? That when I preach the <mark class="chg">gospel,</mark> I may present the gospel without charge, that I may not abuse my authority in the gospel.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the verse τοῦ γὰρ Κυρίου ἡ γῆ καὶ τὸ πλήρωμα αὐτῆς (the earth is the Lord&#x27;s and its fullness); 개역개정 has no equivalent" data-ref="1 Corinthians 10:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 10:28</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the verse τοῦ γὰρ Κυρίου ἡ γῆ καὶ τὸ πλήρωμα αὐτῆς (the earth is the Lord's and its fullness); 개역개정 has no equivalent</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But if anyone says to you, This was offered to idols, do not eat it for the sake of the one who told you, and for conscience' <mark class="chg">sake; for the earth is the Lord's, and all its fullness.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But if anyone says to you, This was offered to idols, do not eat it for the sake of the one who told you, and for conscience' <mark class="chg">sake.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens with the vocative ἀδελφοί (형제들아), which 개역개정 drops" data-ref="1 Corinthians 11:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 11:2</span><span class="srnote">TR opens with the vocative ἀδελφοί (형제들아), which 개역개정 drops</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now I praise <mark class="chg">you, brethren,</mark> that you remember me in all things and keep the traditions just as I delivered them to you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now I praise <mark class="chg">you</mark> that you remember me in all things and keep the traditions just as I delivered them to you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has Λάβετε, φάγετε and τὸ ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν κλώμενον (take, eat; broken for you), both absent from 개역개정" data-ref="1 Corinthians 11:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 11:24</span><span class="srnote">TR has Λάβετε, φάγετε and τὸ ὑπὲρ ὑμῶν κλώμενον (take, eat; broken for you), both absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and when He had given thanks, He broke it and said, <mark class="chg">Take, eat.</mark> This is My body which is <mark class="chg">broken</mark> for you; do this in remembrance of Me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and when He had given thanks, He broke it and said, This is My body which is for you; do this in remembrance of Me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἀναξίως (in an unworthy manner) in this verse; 개역개정 keeps it only in v.27" data-ref="1 Corinthians 11:29"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 11:29</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἀναξίως (in an unworthy manner) in this verse; 개역개정 keeps it only in v.27</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For he who eats and <mark class="chg">drinks in an unworthy manner,</mark> not discerning the Lord's body, eats and drinks judgment to himself.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For he who eats and <mark class="chg">drinks,</mark> not discerning the Lord's body, eats and drinks judgment to himself.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὁ δεύτερος ἄνθρωπος ὁ Κύριος ἐξ οὐρανοῦ (the second Man is the Lord from heaven); 개역개정 omits 주" data-ref="1 Corinthians 15:47"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 15:47</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὁ δεύτερος ἄνθρωπος ὁ Κύριος ἐξ οὐρανοῦ (the second Man is the Lord from heaven); 개역개정 omits 주</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The first man was of the earth, made of dust; the second Man is <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> from heaven.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The first man was of the earth, made of dust; the second Man is from heaven.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR order is Ποῦ σου, Θάνατε, τὸ κέντρον; Ποῦ σου, ᾍδη, τὸ νῖκος; — sting first, then Hades (음부); 개역개정 reverses the clauses and reads 사망아 twice" data-ref="1 Corinthians 15:55"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 15:55</span><span class="srnote">TR order is Ποῦ σου, Θάνατε, τὸ κέντρον; Ποῦ σου, ᾍδη, τὸ νῖκος; — sting first, then Hades (음부); 개역개정 reverses the clauses and reads 사망아 twice</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">O Death, where is your sting? <mark class="chg">O Hades, where is your victory?</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">O Death, where is your victory?</mark> O Death, where is your sting?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τὸν Κύριον Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν; 개역개정 has only 주" data-ref="1 Corinthians 16:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Corinthians 16:22</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τὸν Κύριον Ἰησοῦν Χριστόν; 개역개정 has only 주</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If anyone does not love the <mark class="chg">Lord Jesus Christ,</mark> let him be accursed. O Lord, come!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If anyone does not love the <mark class="chg">Lord,</mark> let him be accursed. O Lord, come!</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 Corinthians</span><span class="bkcount">15</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR repeats καὶ σωτηρίας in the second clause" data-ref="2 Corinthians 1:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 1:6</span><span class="srnote">TR repeats καὶ σωτηρίας in the second clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now if we are afflicted, it is for your comfort and salvation; and if we are comforted, it is for your <mark class="chg">comfort and salvation,</mark> which is effective for enduring the same sufferings which we also suffer.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now if we are afflicted, it is for your comfort and salvation; and if we are comforted, it is for your <mark class="chg">comfort,</mark> which is effective for enduring the same sufferings which we also suffer.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἐν ἁπλότητι καὶ εἰλικρινείᾳ Θεοῦ (simplicity/순전함); 개역개정 follows the critical ἁγιότητι (거룩함)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 1:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 1:12</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἐν ἁπλότητι καὶ εἰλικρινείᾳ Θεοῦ (simplicity/순전함); 개역개정 follows the critical ἁγιότητι (거룩함)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For our boasting is this: the testimony of our conscience that we conducted ourselves in the world in <mark class="chg">simplicity</mark> and godly sincerity, not with fleshly wisdom but by the grace of God, and more abundantly toward you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For our boasting is this: the testimony of our conscience that we conducted ourselves in the world in <mark class="chg">holiness</mark> and godly sincerity, not with fleshly wisdom but by the grace of God, and more abundantly toward you.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τὴν νέκρωσιν τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the dying of the Lord Jesus); 개역개정 has only 예수" data-ref="2 Corinthians 4:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 4:10</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τὴν νέκρωσιν τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the dying of the Lord Jesus); 개역개정 has only 예수</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">always carrying about in the body the dying of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> Jesus, that the life of Jesus also may be manifested in our body.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">always carrying about in the body the dying of Jesus, that the life of Jesus also may be manifested in our body.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads γέγονε καινὰ τὰ πάντα (all things have become new); 개역개정 drops τὰ πάντα" data-ref="2 Corinthians 5:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 5:17</span><span class="srnote">TR reads γέγονε καινὰ τὰ πάντα (all things have become new); 개역개정 drops τὰ πάντα</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore, if anyone is in Christ, he is a new creation; old things have passed away; behold, <mark class="chg">all things have become new.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore, if anyone is in Christ, he is a new creation; old things have passed away; behold, <mark class="chg">the new has come.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has only 그리스도" data-ref="2 Corinthians 5:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 5:18</span><span class="srnote">TR reads διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has only 그리스도</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now all things are of God, who has reconciled us to Himself through <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> Christ and has given us the ministry of reconciliation,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now all things are of God, who has reconciled us to Himself through Christ and has given us the ministry of reconciliation,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὑμεῖς γὰρ ναὸς Θεοῦ ἐστε ζῶντος (you are); 개역개정 follows the critical ἐσμεν (우리는)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 6:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 6:16</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὑμεῖς γὰρ ναὸς Θεοῦ ἐστε ζῶντος (you are); 개역개정 follows the critical ἐσμεν (우리는)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And what agreement has the temple of God with idols? For <mark class="chg">you</mark> are the temple of the living God. As God has said: I will dwell in them and walk among them; I will be their God, and they shall be My people.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And what agreement has the temple of God with idols? For <mark class="chg">we</mark> are the temple of the living God. As God has said: I will dwell in them and walk among them; I will be their God, and they shall be My people.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds τῆς καυχήσεως (&#x27;of this boasting&#x27;)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 9:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 9:4</span><span class="srnote">TR adds τῆς καυχήσεως ('of this boasting')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">lest if some Macedonians come with me and find you unprepared, we, not to mention you, should be ashamed of this <mark class="chg">confident boasting.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">lest if some Macedonians come with me and find you unprepared, we, not to mention you, should be ashamed of this <mark class="chg">confidence.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes οὕτως καὶ ἡμεῖς Χριστοῦ (so we also are Christ&#x27;s); 개역개정 keeps only 우리도 그러한 줄" data-ref="2 Corinthians 10:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 10:7</span><span class="srnote">TR closes οὕτως καὶ ἡμεῖς Χριστοῦ (so we also are Christ's); 개역개정 keeps only 우리도 그러한 줄</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Do you look at things according to the outward appearance? If anyone is convinced in himself that he is Christ's, let him again consider this in himself, that just as he is Christ's, even so we also <mark class="chg">are Christ's.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Do you look at things according to the outward appearance? If anyone is convinced in himself that he is Christ's, let him again consider this in himself, that just as he is Christ's, even so we also <mark class="chg">are.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἀπὸ τῆς ἁπλότητος τῆς εἰς τὸν Χριστόν only; the critical καὶ τῆς ἁγνότητος behind 깨끗함 is absent" data-ref="2 Corinthians 11:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 11:3</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἀπὸ τῆς ἁπλότητος τῆς εἰς τὸν Χριστόν only; the critical καὶ τῆς ἁγνότητος behind 깨끗함 is absent</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But I fear, lest somehow, as the serpent deceived Eve by his craftiness, so your minds may be corrupted from the simplicity of devotion to Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But I fear, lest somehow, as the serpent deceived Eve by his craftiness, so your minds may be corrupted from the simplicity <mark class="chg">and purity</mark> of devotion to Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τοῦ Πατρὸς τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 omits 그리스도" data-ref="2 Corinthians 11:31"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 11:31</span><span class="srnote">TR τοῦ Πατρὸς τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 omits 그리스도</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The God and Father of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> who is blessed forever, knows that I am not lying.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The God and Father of our Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> who is blessed forever, knows that I am not lying.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR καυχᾶσθαι δή (&#x27;boasting indeed&#x27;), not the critical δεῖ (&#x27;it is necessary&#x27;)" data-ref="2 Corinthians 12:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 12:1</span><span class="srnote">TR καυχᾶσθαι δή ('boasting indeed'), not the critical δεῖ ('it is necessary')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Though it is not profitable, I <mark class="chg">do</mark> boast; I will speak of visions and revelations of the Lord.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Though it is not profitable, I <mark class="chg">must needs</mark> boast; I will speak of visions and revelations of the Lord.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="the kiss clause is TR 13:12, not part of 13:11" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:11</span><span class="srnote">the kiss clause is TR 13:12, not part of 13:11</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Finally, brethren, rejoice. Become complete. Be of good comfort, be of one mind, live in peace; and the God of love and peace will be with you.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Finally, brethren, rejoice. Become complete. Be of good comfort, be of one mind, live in peace; and the God of love and peace will be with you. <mark class="chg">Greet one another with a holy kiss.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR 13:12; 개역개정 folded this into 13:11" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:12</span><span class="srnote">TR 13:12; 개역개정 folded this into 13:11</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Greet one another with a holy kiss.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">All the saints greet you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR 13:13; the grace benediction moves to a new 13:14" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:13</span><span class="srnote">TR 13:13; the grace benediction moves to a new 13:14</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">All</mark> the <mark class="chg">saints greet you.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The grace of</mark> the <mark class="chg">Lord Jesus Christ, and the love of God, and the communion of the Holy Spirit be with you all.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR numbers the grace benediction as 13:14; Korean had it as 13:13" data-ref="2 Corinthians 13:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Corinthians 13:14</span><span class="srnote">TR numbers the grace benediction as 13:14; Korean had it as 13:13</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="rest">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The grace of the Lord Jesus Christ, and the love of God, and the communion of the Holy Spirit be with you all.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><span class="gone">(not present)</span></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Galatians</span><span class="bkcount">13</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ὅτε δὲ εὐδόκησεν ὁ Θεός (when God was pleased) — the subject 하나님 is dropped in 개역개정" data-ref="Galatians 1:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 1:15</span><span class="srnote">TR ὅτε δὲ εὐδόκησεν ὁ Θεός (when God was pleased) — the subject 하나님 is dropped in 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But when it pleased <mark class="chg">God,</mark> who set me apart from my mother's womb and called me through His grace,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But when it pleased <mark class="chg">Him</mark> who set me apart from my mother's womb and called me through His grace,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἱστορῆσαι Πέτρον (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶν behind 게바" data-ref="Galatians 1:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 1:18</span><span class="srnote">TR ἱστορῆσαι Πέτρον (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶν behind 게바</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then after three years I went up to Jerusalem to see <mark class="chg">Peter,</mark> and remained with him fifteen days.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then after three years I went up to Jerusalem to see <mark class="chg">Cephas,</mark> and remained with him fifteen days.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ὅτε δὲ ἦλθεν Πέτρος (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶς behind 게바" data-ref="Galatians 2:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 2:11</span><span class="srnote">TR ὅτε δὲ ἦλθεν Πέτρος (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾶς behind 게바</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now when <mark class="chg">Peter had come</mark> to Antioch, I withstood him to his face, because he was to be blamed.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now when <mark class="chg">Cephas came</mark> to Antioch, I withstood him to his face, because he was to be blamed.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR εἶπεν τῷ Πέτρῳ (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾷ behind 게바" data-ref="Galatians 2:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 2:14</span><span class="srnote">TR εἶπεν τῷ Πέτρῳ (Peter), not the critical Κηφᾷ behind 게바</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But when I saw that they were not straightforward about the truth of the gospel, I said to <mark class="chg">Peter</mark> before them all, If you, being a Jew, live in the manner of Gentiles and not as the Jews, why do you compel Gentiles to live as Jews?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But when I saw that they were not straightforward about the truth of the gospel, I said to <mark class="chg">Cephas</mark> before them all, If you, being a Jew, live in the manner of Gentiles and not as the Jews, why do you compel Gentiles to live as Jews?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἐν ὑμῖν (&#x27;among you&#x27;) and τῇ ἀληθεία μὴ πείθεσθαι (&#x27;not obey the truth&#x27;)" data-ref="Galatians 3:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 3:1</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἐν ὑμῖν ('among you') and τῇ ἀληθεία μὴ πείθεσθαι ('not obey the truth')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">O foolish Galatians! Who has bewitched <mark class="chg">you that you should not obey the truth,</mark> before whose eyes Jesus Christ was clearly portrayed <mark class="chg">among you</mark> as crucified?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">O foolish Galatians! Who has bewitched <mark class="chg">you,</mark> before whose eyes Jesus Christ was clearly portrayed as crucified?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τὴν διαθήκην τὴν προκεκυρωμένην ὑπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ εἰς Χριστόν (confirmed of God in Christ); 개역개정 omits 그리스도 안에서" data-ref="Galatians 3:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 3:17</span><span class="srnote">TR τὴν διαθήκην τὴν προκεκυρωμένην ὑπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ εἰς Χριστόν (confirmed of God in Christ); 개역개정 omits 그리스도 안에서</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And this I say, that the law, which was four hundred and thirty years later, cannot annul the covenant that was confirmed before by <mark class="chg">God in Christ,</mark> so as to make the promise of no effect.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And this I say, that the law, which was four hundred and thirty years later, cannot annul the covenant that was confirmed before by <mark class="chg">God,</mark> so as to make the promise of no effect.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR κληρονόμος Θεοῦ διὰ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 follows the critical διὰ θεοῦ (하나님으로 말미암아)" data-ref="Galatians 4:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 4:7</span><span class="srnote">TR κληρονόμος Θεοῦ διὰ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 follows the critical διὰ θεοῦ (하나님으로 말미암아)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore you are no longer a slave but a son, and if a son, then an heir <mark class="chg">of God</mark> through <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore you are no longer a slave but a son, and if a son, then an heir through <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τίς (&#x27;what then was&#x27;), not the critical ποῦ (&#x27;where&#x27;)" data-ref="Galatians 4:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 4:15</span><span class="srnote">TR τίς ('what then was'), not the critical ποῦ ('where')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">What</mark> then <mark class="chg">has become of</mark> your blessing? For I bear you witness that, if possible, you would have plucked out your own eyes and given them to me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">Where</mark> then <mark class="chg">is</mark> your blessing? For I bear you witness that, if possible, you would have plucked out your own eyes and given them to me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR μήτηρ πάντων ἡμῶν (mother of us all); 개역개정 has only 우리 어머니" data-ref="Galatians 4:26"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 4:26</span><span class="srnote">TR μήτηρ πάντων ἡμῶν (mother of us all); 개역개정 has only 우리 어머니</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">but the Jerusalem above is free, which is <mark class="chg">the mother of us all.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">but the Jerusalem above is free, which is <mark class="chg">our mother.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἅτινά ἐστι μοιχεία, πορνεία, ἀκαθαρσία, ἀσέλγεια — μοιχεία (간음) is absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Galatians 5:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 5:19</span><span class="srnote">TR ἅτινά ἐστι μοιχεία, πορνεία, ἀκαθαρσία, ἀσέλγεια — μοιχεία (간음) is absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now the works of the flesh are evident, which are: <mark class="chg">adultery,</mark> fornication, uncleanness, lewdness,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now the works of the flesh are evident, which are: fornication, uncleanness, lewdness,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR φθόνοι, φόνοι, μέθαι — φόνοι (살인) is absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Galatians 5:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 5:21</span><span class="srnote">TR φθόνοι, φόνοι, μέθαι — φόνοι (살인) is absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">envy, <mark class="chg">murders,</mark> drunkenness, revelries, and the like; of which I tell you beforehand, just as I also told you in time past, that those who practice such things will not inherit the kingdom of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">envy, drunkenness, revelries, and the like; of which I tell you beforehand, just as I also told you in time past, that those who practice such things will not inherit the kingdom of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐν γὰρ Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ οὔτε περιτομή τι ἰσχύει; the opening 그리스도 예수 안에서는 is missing" data-ref="Galatians 6:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 6:15</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐν γὰρ Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ οὔτε περιτομή τι ἰσχύει; the opening 그리스도 예수 안에서는 is missing</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For <mark class="chg">in Christ Jesus</mark> neither circumcision nor uncircumcision <mark class="chg">avails</mark> anything, but a new creation.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For neither circumcision nor uncircumcision <mark class="chg">is</mark> anything, but a new creation.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τὰ στίγματα τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the marks of the Lord Jesus); 개역개정 keeps only 예수" data-ref="Galatians 6:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Galatians 6:17</span><span class="srnote">TR τὰ στίγματα τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ (the marks of the Lord Jesus); 개역개정 keeps only 예수</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">From now on let no one trouble me, for I bear in my body the marks of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> Jesus.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">From now on let no one trouble me, for I bear in my body the marks of Jesus.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Ephesians</span><span class="bkcount">14</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τοὺς ὀφθαλμοὺς τῆς διανοίας (understanding), not the critical τῆς καρδίας behind 마음의" data-ref="Ephesians 1:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 1:18</span><span class="srnote">TR τοὺς ὀφθαλμοὺς τῆς διανοίας (understanding), not the critical τῆς καρδίας behind 마음의</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">the eyes of your <mark class="chg">understanding</mark> being enlightened, that you may know what is the hope of His calling, and what are the riches of the glory of His inheritance in the saints,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">the eyes of your <mark class="chg">heart</mark> being enlightened, that you may know what is the hope of His calling, and what are the riches of the glory of His inheritance in the saints,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR εὐηγγελίσατο εἰρήνην once; 개역개정 doubles 평안을 from the critical double εἰρήνην" data-ref="Ephesians 2:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 2:17</span><span class="srnote">TR εὐηγγελίσατο εἰρήνην once; 개역개정 doubles 평안을 from the critical double εἰρήνην</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And He came and preached peace to you who were afar off and to those who were near.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And He came and preached peace to you who were afar off and <mark class="chg">peace</mark> to those who were near.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐν τῷ Χριστῷ (in Christ); the 예수 of 그리스도 예수 is the critical Ἰησοῦ" data-ref="Ephesians 3:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:6</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐν τῷ Χριστῷ (in Christ); the 예수 of 그리스도 예수 is the critical Ἰησοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that the Gentiles should be fellow heirs, of the same body, and partakers of His promise in Christ through the gospel,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that the Gentiles should be fellow heirs, of the same body, and partakers of His promise in Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> through the gospel,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τίς ἡ κοινωνία τοῦ μυστηρίου (the fellowship of the mystery) and τῷ τὰ πάντα κτίσαντι διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has 경륜 and no 예수 그리스도로 말미암아" data-ref="Ephesians 3:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:9</span><span class="srnote">TR τίς ἡ κοινωνία τοῦ μυστηρίου (the fellowship of the mystery) and τῷ τὰ πάντα κτίσαντι διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has 경륜 and no 예수 그리스도로 말미암아</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and to make all see what is the <mark class="chg">fellowship</mark> of the mystery which has been hidden from the ages in God who created all <mark class="chg">things through Jesus Christ,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and to make all see what is the <mark class="chg">dispensation</mark> of the mystery which has been hidden from the ages in God who created all <mark class="chg">things,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ" data-ref="Ephesians 3:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:14</span><span class="srnote">TR has τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For this reason I bow my knees to <mark class="chg">the Father of our Lord Jesus Christ,</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For this reason I bow my knees to <mark class="chg">every family in heaven and on earth,</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="re-cut so that 3:14 carries &#x27;to the Father of our Lord Jesus Christ&#x27;" data-ref="Ephesians 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 3:15</span><span class="srnote">re-cut so that 3:14 carries 'to the Father of our Lord Jesus Christ'</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-move" data-k="move">moved</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">to</mark> the Father <mark class="chg">who gives name to</mark> every family in heaven and on <mark class="chg">earth,</mark> I pray,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">before</mark> the Father <mark class="chg">from whom</mark> every family in heaven and on <mark class="chg">earth is named,</mark> I <mark class="chg">bow my knees and</mark> pray,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR καὶ ἐν πᾶσιν ὑμῖν (and in you all); 개역개정&#x27;s 만유 가운데 drops the ὑμῖν" data-ref="Ephesians 4:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 4:6</span><span class="srnote">TR καὶ ἐν πᾶσιν ὑμῖν (and in you all); 개역개정's 만유 가운데 drops the ὑμῖν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">one God and Father of all, who is above all, and through all, and in <mark class="chg">you</mark> all.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">one God and Father of all, who is above all, and through all, and in all.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR καὶ κατέβη πρῶτον (first descended); 개역개정 omits 먼저" data-ref="Ephesians 4:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 4:9</span><span class="srnote">TR καὶ κατέβη πρῶτον (first descended); 개역개정 omits 먼저</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now this, that He ascended, what does it mean but that He also <mark class="chg">first</mark> descended into the lower parts of the earth?</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now this, that He ascended, what does it mean but that He also descended into the lower parts of the earth?</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR καθὼς καὶ τὰ λοιπὰ ἔθνη (as the rest of the Gentiles); 개역개정 has only 이방인" data-ref="Ephesians 4:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 4:17</span><span class="srnote">TR καθὼς καὶ τὰ λοιπὰ ἔθνη (as the rest of the Gentiles); 개역개정 has only 이방인</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This I say, therefore, and testify in the Lord, that you should no longer walk as the <mark class="chg">rest of the</mark> Gentiles walk, in the futility of their mind,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This I say, therefore, and testify in the Lord, that you should no longer walk as the Gentiles walk, in the futility of their mind,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ὁ καρπὸς τοῦ Πνεύματος (fruit of the Spirit), not the critical τοῦ φωτός behind 빛의" data-ref="Ephesians 5:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 5:9</span><span class="srnote">TR ὁ καρπὸς τοῦ Πνεύματος (fruit of the Spirit), not the critical τοῦ φωτός behind 빛의</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for the fruit of <mark class="chg">the Spirit</mark> is in all goodness, righteousness, and truth,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for the fruit of <mark class="chg">light</mark> is in all goodness, righteousness, and truth,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐν φόβῳ Θεοῦ (fear of God), not the critical Χριστοῦ behind 그리스도를" data-ref="Ephesians 5:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 5:21</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐν φόβῳ Θεοῦ (fear of God), not the critical Χριστοῦ behind 그리스도를</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">submitting to one another in the fear of <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">submitting to one another in the fear of <mark class="chg">Christ.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἐκ τῆς σαρκὸς αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐκ τῶν ὀστεων αὐτοῦ (of His flesh and of His bones); 개역개정 drops the clause" data-ref="Ephesians 5:30"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 5:30</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἐκ τῆς σαρκὸς αὐτοῦ καὶ ἐκ τῶν ὀστεων αὐτοῦ (of His flesh and of His bones); 개역개정 drops the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For we are members of His <mark class="chg">body, of His flesh and of His bones.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For we are members of His <mark class="chg">body.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads Τὸ λοιπόν, ἀδελφοί μου (Finally, my brethren); 개역개정 omits the address 형제들아" data-ref="Ephesians 6:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 6:10</span><span class="srnote">TR reads Τὸ λοιπόν, ἀδελφοί μου (Finally, my brethren); 개역개정 omits the address 형제들아</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Finally, <mark class="chg">my brethren,</mark> be strong in the Lord and in the power of His might.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Finally, be strong in the Lord and in the power of His might.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Ephesians 6:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Ephesians 6:24</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Grace be with all those who love our Lord Jesus Christ in sincerity. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Grace be with all those who love our Lord Jesus Christ in sincerity.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Philippians</span><span class="bkcount">6</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR v16 is the ἐριθείας clause (selfish ambition); 개역개정 follows the critical text, which puts the ἀγάπης clause first and so swaps vv.16-17" data-ref="Philippians 1:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 1:16</span><span class="srnote">TR v16 is the ἐριθείας clause (selfish ambition); 개역개정 follows the critical text, which puts the ἀγάπης clause first and so swaps vv.16-17</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The <mark class="chg">former preach Christ from selfish ambition, not sincerely, supposing to add affliction to my chains.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The <mark class="chg">latter do it out of love, knowing that I am appointed for the defense of the gospel.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR v17 is the ἀγάπης clause (out of love); 개역개정 carries the critical text&#x27;s reversed order" data-ref="Philippians 1:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 1:17</span><span class="srnote">TR v17 is the ἀγάπης clause (out of love); 개역개정 carries the critical text's reversed order</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">But the latter do it</mark> out of <mark class="chg">love, knowing that I am appointed for the defense of the gospel.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx"><mark class="chg">The others preach Christ</mark> out of <mark class="chg">selfish ambition, not sincerely, supposing to add affliction to my chains.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR πνεύματι Θεῷ (&#x27;worship God in the Spirit&#x27;), not the critical πνεύματι θεοῦ" data-ref="Philippians 3:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 3:3</span><span class="srnote">TR πνεύματι Θεῷ ('worship God in the Spirit'), not the critical πνεύματι θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For we are the circumcision, who <mark class="chg">worship God in</mark> the <mark class="chg">Spirit,</mark> rejoice in Christ Jesus, and have no confidence in the flesh,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For we are the circumcision, who <mark class="chg">serve by</mark> the <mark class="chg">Spirit of God and</mark> rejoice in Christ Jesus, and have no confidence in the flesh,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds κανόνι, τὸ αὐτὸ φρονεῖν (by the same rule, be of the same mind), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Philippians 3:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 3:16</span><span class="srnote">TR adds κανόνι, τὸ αὐτὸ φρονεῖν (by the same rule, be of the same mind), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Nevertheless, to whatever extent we have already attained, let us walk by the same <mark class="chg">rule and be of the same mind.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Nevertheless, to whatever extent we have already attained, let us walk by the same <mark class="chg">rule.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἐν τῷ ἐνδυναμοῦντί με Χριστῷ; 개역개정 drops Χριστῷ, leaving the strengthener unnamed" data-ref="Philippians 4:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 4:13</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἐν τῷ ἐνδυναμοῦντί με Χριστῷ; 개역개정 drops Χριστῷ, leaving the strengthener unnamed</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I can do all things through <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> who strengthens me.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I can do all things through <mark class="chg">Him</mark> who strengthens me.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads μετὰ πάντων ὑμῶν (with you all) plus Ἀμήν; 개역개정 follows the critical μετὰ τοῦ πνεύματος ὑμῶν (너희 심령에) and has no 아멘" data-ref="Philippians 4:23"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Philippians 4:23</span><span class="srnote">TR reads μετὰ πάντων ὑμῶν (with you all) plus Ἀμήν; 개역개정 follows the critical μετὰ τοῦ πνεύματος ὑμῶν (너희 심령에) and has no 아멘</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with <mark class="chg">you all. Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with <mark class="chg">your spirit.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">Colossians</span><span class="bkcount">14</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 drops the second source, 주 예수 그리스도" data-ref="Colossians 1:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 1:2</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 drops the second source, 주 예수 그리스도</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">To the saints and faithful brethren in Christ who are in Colosse: Grace to you and peace from God our <mark class="chg">Father and the Lord Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">To the saints and faithful brethren in Christ who are in Colosse: Grace to you and peace from God our <mark class="chg">Father.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἔστι καρποφορούμενον only; αὐξανόμενον (자라는) is a critical-text addition kept by 개역개정" data-ref="Colossians 1:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 1:6</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἔστι καρποφορούμενον only; αὐξανόμενον (자라는) is a critical-text addition kept by 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">which has come to you, as it is also in all the world, and is bearing <mark class="chg">fruit,</mark> as it also does among you, since the day you heard and knew the grace of God in truth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">which has come to you, as it is also in all the world, and is bearing <mark class="chg">fruit and increasing,</mark> as it also does among you, since the day you heard and knew the grace of God in truth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has διὰ τοῦ αἵματος αὐτοῦ (through His blood), absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Colossians 1:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 1:14</span><span class="srnote">TR has διὰ τοῦ αἵματος αὐτοῦ (through His blood), absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">in whom we have <mark class="chg">redemption through His blood,</mark> the forgiveness of sins.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">in whom we have <mark class="chg">redemption,</mark> the forgiveness of sins.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τοῦ μυστηρίου τοῦ Θεοῦ καὶ πατρὸς καὶ τοῦ Χριστοῦ (God, both the Father and Christ); 개역개정 drops 아버지" data-ref="Colossians 2:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 2:2</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τοῦ μυστηρίου τοῦ Θεοῦ καὶ πατρὸς καὶ τοῦ Χριστοῦ (God, both the Father and Christ); 개역개정 drops 아버지</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that their hearts may be encouraged, being knit together in love, and attaining to all riches of the full assurance of understanding, to the knowledge of the mystery of God, <mark class="chg">both of the Father and of</mark> Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that their hearts may be encouraged, being knit together in love, and attaining to all riches of the full assurance of understanding, to the knowledge of the mystery of God, <mark class="chg">which is</mark> Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τοῦ σώματος τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν τῆς σαρκός (the body of the sins of the flesh); 개역개정 drops τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν (죄)" data-ref="Colossians 2:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 2:11</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τοῦ σώματος τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν τῆς σαρκός (the body of the sins of the flesh); 개역개정 drops τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν (죄)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">In Him you were also circumcised with the circumcision made without hands, by putting off the body of the <mark class="chg">sins of the</mark> flesh, by the circumcision of Christ,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">In Him you were also circumcised with the circumcision made without hands, by putting off the body of the flesh, by the circumcision of Christ,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads χαρισάμενος ὑμῖν (forgiven you), not the critical ἡμῖν that 개역개정 renders 우리의" data-ref="Colossians 2:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 2:13</span><span class="srnote">TR reads χαρισάμενος ὑμῖν (forgiven you), not the critical ἡμῖν that 개역개정 renders 우리의</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And you, being dead in your trespasses and the uncircumcision of your flesh, He has made alive together with Him, having forgiven <mark class="chg">you</mark> all trespasses,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And you, being dead in your trespasses and the uncircumcision of your flesh, He has made alive together with Him, having forgiven <mark class="chg">us</mark> all trespasses,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὁ Χριστός (Christ), not the critical ὁ κύριος that 개역개정 renders 주께서" data-ref="Colossians 3:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:13</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὁ Χριστός (Christ), not the critical ὁ κύριος that 개역개정 renders 주께서</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">bearing with one another, and forgiving one another, if anyone has a complaint against another; even as <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> forgave you, so you also must do.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">bearing with one another, and forgiving one another, if anyone has a complaint against another; even as <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> forgave you, so you also must do.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἡ εἰρήνη τοῦ Θεοῦ (the peace of God); 개역개정 follows the critical τοῦ Χριστοῦ (그리스도의 평강)" data-ref="Colossians 3:15"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:15</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἡ εἰρήνη τοῦ Θεοῦ (the peace of God); 개역개정 follows the critical τοῦ Χριστοῦ (그리스도의 평강)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And let the peace of <mark class="chg">God</mark> rule in your hearts, to which also you were called in one body; and be thankful.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And let the peace of <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> rule in your hearts, to which also you were called in one body; and be thankful.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ᾄδοντες … τῷ Κυρίῳ (singing to the Lord); 개역개정 follows the critical τῷ θεῷ (하나님을 찬양하고)" data-ref="Colossians 3:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:16</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ᾄδοντες … τῷ Κυρίῳ (singing to the Lord); 개역개정 follows the critical τῷ θεῷ (하나님을 찬양하고)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let the word of Christ dwell in you richly in all wisdom, teaching and admonishing one another in psalms and hymns and spiritual songs, singing with grace in your hearts to <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let the word of Christ dwell in you richly in all wisdom, teaching and admonishing one another in psalms and hymns and spiritual songs, singing with grace in your hearts to <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads φοβούμενοι τὸν Θεόν (fearing God); 개역개정 follows the critical τὸν κύριον (주를 두려워하여)" data-ref="Colossians 3:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 3:22</span><span class="srnote">TR reads φοβούμενοι τὸν Θεόν (fearing God); 개역개정 follows the critical τὸν κύριον (주를 두려워하여)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Bondservants, obey in all things your masters according to the flesh, not with eyeservice, as men-pleasers, but in sincerity of heart, fearing <mark class="chg">God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Bondservants, obey in all things your masters according to the flesh, not with eyeservice, as men-pleasers, but in sincerity of heart, fearing <mark class="chg">the Lord.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἵνα γνῷ τὰ περὶ ὑμῶν (that he may know your circumstances); 개역개정 follows the critical γνῶτε and reverses subject and object (너희로 우리 사정을)" data-ref="Colossians 4:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:8</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἵνα γνῷ τὰ περὶ ὑμῶν (that he may know your circumstances); 개역개정 follows the critical γνῶτε and reverses subject and object (너희로 우리 사정을)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I am sending him to you for this very purpose, that <mark class="chg">he</mark> may know <mark class="chg">your</mark> circumstances and comfort your hearts.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I am sending him to you for this very purpose, that <mark class="chg">you</mark> may know <mark class="chg">our</mark> circumstances and <mark class="chg">that he may</mark> comfort your hearts.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τέλειοι καὶ πεπληρωμένοι (&#x27;perfect and filled&#x27;), not πεπληροφορημένοι (&#x27;fully assured&#x27;)" data-ref="Colossians 4:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:12</span><span class="srnote">TR τέλειοι καὶ πεπληρωμένοι ('perfect and filled'), not πεπληροφορημένοι ('fully assured')</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Epaphras, who is one of your number, a servant of Christ Jesus, greets you, always laboring fervently for you in prayers, that you may stand perfect and <mark class="chg">complete</mark> in all the will of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Epaphras, who is one of your number, a servant of Christ Jesus, greets you, always laboring fervently for you in prayers, that you may stand perfect and <mark class="chg">fully assured</mark> in all the will of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ζῆλον πολύν (great zeal), not the critical πόνον that 개역개정 renders 많이 수고하는" data-ref="Colossians 4:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:13</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ζῆλον πολύν (great zeal), not the critical πόνον that 개역개정 renders 많이 수고하는</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For I bear him witness that he <mark class="chg">has a great zeal</mark> for you, and for those who are in Laodicea, and those in Hierapolis.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For I bear him witness that he <mark class="chg">labors much</mark> for you, and for those who are in Laodicea, and those in Hierapolis.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from 개역개정" data-ref="Colossians 4:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Colossians 4:18</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">This salutation by my own hand—Paul. Remember my chains. Grace be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">This salutation by my own hand—Paul. Remember my chains. Grace be with you.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 Thessalonians</span><span class="bkcount">5</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ after the greeting; 개역개정 drops the source of grace and peace" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 1:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 1:1</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἀπὸ Θεοῦ πατρὸς ἡμῶν καὶ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ after the greeting; 개역개정 drops the source of grace and peace</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Paul, Silvanus, and Timothy, To the church of the Thessalonians in God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ: Grace to you and <mark class="chg">peace from God our Father and the Lord Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Paul, Silvanus, and Timothy, To the church of the Thessalonians in God the Father and the Lord Jesus Christ: Grace to you and <mark class="chg">peace.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has καὶ συνεργὸν ἡμῶν (and our fellow laborer); 개역개정 drops 우리의 동역자" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 3:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 3:2</span><span class="srnote">TR has καὶ συνεργὸν ἡμῶν (and our fellow laborer); 개역개정 drops 우리의 동역자</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and sent Timothy, our brother and minister of <mark class="chg">God, and our fellow laborer</mark> in the gospel of Christ, to establish you and encourage you concerning your faith,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and sent Timothy, our brother and minister of <mark class="chg">God</mark> in the gospel of Christ, to establish you and encourage you concerning your faith,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads εἰς ἡμᾶς (given to us), not the critical εἰς ὑμᾶς that 개역개정 renders 너희에게" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 4:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 4:8</span><span class="srnote">TR reads εἰς ἡμᾶς (given to us), not the critical εἰς ὑμᾶς that 개역개정 renders 너희에게</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Therefore he who rejects this does not reject man, but God, who has also given His Holy Spirit to <mark class="chg">us.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Therefore he who rejects this does not reject man, but God, who has also given His Holy Spirit to <mark class="chg">you.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads πᾶσι τοῖς ἁγίοις ἀδελφοῖς (all the holy brethren); 개역개정 drops ἁγίοις" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 5:27"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 5:27</span><span class="srnote">TR reads πᾶσι τοῖς ἁγίοις ἀδελφοῖς (all the holy brethren); 개역개정 drops ἁγίοις</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I charge you by the Lord that this epistle be read to all the <mark class="chg">holy</mark> brethren.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I charge you by the Lord that this epistle be read to all the brethren.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from 개역개정" data-ref="1 Thessalonians 5:28"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Thessalonians 5:28</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the letter with Ἀμήν, absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 Thessalonians</span><span class="bkcount">5</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἡμέρα τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the day of Christ), not the critical τοῦ κυρίου that 개역개정 renders 주의 날" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 2:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 2:2</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἡμέρα τοῦ Χριστοῦ (the day of Christ), not the critical τοῦ κυρίου that 개역개정 renders 주의 날</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">that you not be soon shaken in mind or troubled, either by spirit or by word or by letter, as if from us, as though the day of <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> had come.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">that you not be soon shaken in mind or troubled, either by spirit or by word or by letter, as if from us, as though the day of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> had come.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὁ ἄνθρωπος τῆς ἁμαρτίας (the man of sin); 개역개정 follows the critical τῆς ἀνομίας (불법의 사람)" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 2:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 2:3</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὁ ἄνθρωπος τῆς ἁμαρτίας (the man of sin); 개역개정 follows the critical τῆς ἀνομίας (불법의 사람)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let no one deceive you by any means; for that Day will not come unless the falling away comes first, and the man of <mark class="chg">sin</mark> is revealed, the son of perdition,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let no one deceive you by any means; for that Day will not come unless the falling away comes first, and the man of <mark class="chg">lawlessness</mark> is revealed, the son of perdition,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ὡς Θεὸν καθίσαι (sit as God); 개역개정 drops ὡς θεόν" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 2:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 2:4</span><span class="srnote">TR has ὡς Θεὸν καθίσαι (sit as God); 개역개정 drops ὡς θεόν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">who opposes and exalts himself above all that is called God or that is worshiped, so that he sits <mark class="chg">as God</mark> in the temple of God, showing himself that he is God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">who opposes and exalts himself above all that is called God or that is worshiped, so that he sits in the temple of God, showing himself that he is God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads διὰ τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (through our Lord Jesus Christ); 개역개정 follows the critical ἐν κυρίῳ (안에서)" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 3:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 3:12</span><span class="srnote">TR reads διὰ τοῦ Κυρίου ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (through our Lord Jesus Christ); 개역개정 follows the critical ἐν κυρίῳ (안에서)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now those who are such we command and exhort <mark class="chg">through our</mark> Lord Jesus Christ that they work in quietness and eat their own bread.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now those who are such we command and exhort <mark class="chg">in the</mark> Lord Jesus Christ that they work in quietness and eat their own bread.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes the epistle with Ἀμήν; 개역개정 omits it (the Πρὸς Θεσσαλονικεῖς colophon is a subscription, not verse text)" data-ref="2 Thessalonians 3:18"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Thessalonians 3:18</span><span class="srnote">TR closes the epistle with Ἀμήν; 개역개정 omits it (the Πρὸς Θεσσαλονικεῖς colophon is a subscription, not verse text)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of our Lord Jesus Christ be with you all.</span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">1 Timothy</span><span class="bkcount">12</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR μόνῳ σοφῷ Θεῷ (alone wise God); 개역개정&#x27;s 홀로 하나이신 drops σοφῷ" data-ref="1 Timothy 1:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 1:17</span><span class="srnote">TR μόνῳ σοφῷ Θεῷ (alone wise God); 개역개정's 홀로 하나이신 drops σοφῷ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Now to the King eternal, immortal, invisible, the only <mark class="chg">wise</mark> God, be honor and glory forever and ever. Amen.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Now to the King eternal, immortal, invisible, the only God, be honor and glory forever and ever. Amen.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἀλήθειαν λέγω ἐν Χριστῷ (I speak the truth in Christ); 개역개정 drops ἐν Χριστῷ" data-ref="1 Timothy 2:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 2:7</span><span class="srnote">TR ἀλήθειαν λέγω ἐν Χριστῷ (I speak the truth in Christ); 개역개정 drops ἐν Χριστῷ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">for which I was appointed a preacher and an apostle—I am speaking the <mark class="chg">truth in Christ,</mark> not lying—a teacher of the Gentiles in faith and truth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">for which I was appointed a preacher and an apostle—I am speaking the <mark class="chg">truth,</mark> not lying—a teacher of the Gentiles in faith and truth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has both μὴ αἰσχροκερδῆ and ἀφιλάργυρον; 개역개정 keeps only the second (돈을 사랑하지 아니하며)" data-ref="1 Timothy 3:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 3:3</span><span class="srnote">TR has both μὴ αἰσχροκερδῆ and ἀφιλάργυρον; 개역개정 keeps only the second (돈을 사랑하지 아니하며)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">not given to wine, not violent, <mark class="chg">not greedy for money,</mark> but gentle, not quarrelsome, not covetous;</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">not given to wine, not violent, but gentle, not quarrelsome, not covetous;</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Θεός (God), critical ὅς; Korean 그는 is the critical reading" data-ref="1 Timothy 3:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 3:16</span><span class="srnote">TR Θεός (God), critical ὅς; Korean 그는 is the critical reading</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And without controversy great is the mystery of godliness: <mark class="chg">God</mark> was manifested in the flesh, justified in the Spirit, seen by angels, preached among the Gentiles, believed on in the world, received up in glory.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And without controversy great is the mystery of godliness: <mark class="chg">He</mark> was manifested in the flesh, justified in the Spirit, seen by angels, preached among the Gentiles, believed on in the world, received up in glory.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has ἐν πνεύματι between 사랑 and 믿음" data-ref="1 Timothy 4:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 4:12</span><span class="srnote">TR has ἐν πνεύματι between 사랑 and 믿음</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Let no one despise your youth, but be an example to the believers in word, in conduct, in love, in <mark class="chg">spirit, in</mark> faith, in purity.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Let no one despise your youth, but be an example to the believers in word, in conduct, in love, in faith, in purity.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τοῦτο γάρ ἐστι καλὸν καὶ ἀπόδεκτον (good and acceptable); 개역개정 keeps only 받으실 만한" data-ref="1 Timothy 5:4"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 5:4</span><span class="srnote">TR τοῦτο γάρ ἐστι καλὸν καὶ ἀπόδεκτον (good and acceptable); 개역개정 keeps only 받으실 만한</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">But if any widow has children or grandchildren, let them first learn to show piety at home and to repay their parents; for this is <mark class="chg">good and</mark> acceptable before God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">But if any widow has children or grandchildren, let them first learn to show piety at home and to repay their parents; for this is acceptable before God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR πιστὸς ἢ πιστή (believing man or woman); 개역개정 has 믿는 여자 only" data-ref="1 Timothy 5:16"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 5:16</span><span class="srnote">TR πιστὸς ἢ πιστή (believing man or woman); 개역개정 has 믿는 여자 only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If any believing <mark class="chg">man or</mark> woman has widows, let <mark class="chg">them</mark> relieve them, and let the church not be burdened, that it may relieve those who are really widows.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If any believing woman has widows, let <mark class="chg">her</mark> relieve them, and let the church not be burdened, that it may relieve those who are really widows.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (Lord Jesus Christ); 개역개정 has 그리스도 예수, dropping 주 and the order" data-ref="1 Timothy 5:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 5:21</span><span class="srnote">TR Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ (Lord Jesus Christ); 개역개정 has 그리스도 예수, dropping 주 and the order</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I charge you before God and <mark class="chg">the Lord Jesus</mark> Christ and the elect angels that you observe these things without prejudice, doing nothing with partiality.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I charge you before God and Christ <mark class="chg">Jesus</mark> and the elect angels that you observe these things without prejudice, doing nothing with partiality.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Ἀφίστασο ἀπὸ τῶν τοιούτων (from such withdraw yourself) is absent from 개역개정" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:5</span><span class="srnote">TR Ἀφίστασο ἀπὸ τῶν τοιούτων (from such withdraw yourself) is absent from 개역개정</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">useless wranglings of men of corrupt minds and destitute of the truth, who suppose that godliness is a means of gain. <mark class="chg">From such withdraw yourself.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">useless wranglings of men of corrupt minds and destitute of the truth, who suppose that godliness is a means of gain.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τῷ Θεῷ τῷ ζῶντι (the living God); 개역개정 drops τῷ ζῶντι" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:17</span><span class="srnote">TR ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τῷ Θεῷ τῷ ζῶντι (the living God); 개역개정 drops τῷ ζῶντι</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Command those who are rich in this present age not to be haughty, nor to trust in uncertain riches but in <mark class="chg">the living</mark> God, who gives us richly all things to enjoy.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Command those who are rich in this present age not to be haughty, nor to trust in uncertain riches but in God, who gives us richly all things to enjoy.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τῆς αἰωνίου ζωῆς (eternal life); 개역개정&#x27;s 참된 생명 follows the critical ὄντως" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:19</span><span class="srnote">TR τῆς αἰωνίου ζωῆς (eternal life); 개역개정's 참된 생명 follows the critical ὄντως</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">storing up for themselves a good foundation for the time to come, that they may lay hold on <mark class="chg">eternal</mark> life.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">storing up for themselves a good foundation for the time to come, that they may lay hold on <mark class="chg">that which is truly</mark> life.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Ἡ χάρις μετὰ σοῦ (with you, singular) plus closing Ἀμήν; 개역개정 has 너희와 and no 아멘" data-ref="1 Timothy 6:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">1 Timothy 6:21</span><span class="srnote">TR Ἡ χάρις μετὰ σοῦ (with you, singular) plus closing Ἀμήν; 개역개정 has 너희와 and no 아멘</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">which some professing have erred concerning the faith. Grace be with <mark class="chg">you. Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">which some professing have erred concerning the faith. Grace be with <mark class="chg">you all.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tbody class="bk">
<tr class="book"><td colspan="3"><span class="bkname">2 Timothy</span><span class="bkcount">5</span></td></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR διδάσκαλος ἐθνῶν (teacher of the Gentiles); 개역개정 drops 이방인의" data-ref="2 Timothy 1:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 1:11</span><span class="srnote">TR διδάσκαλος ἐθνῶν (teacher of the Gentiles); 개역개정 drops 이방인의</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">to which I was appointed a preacher, an apostle, and a <mark class="chg">teacher of the Gentiles.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">to which I was appointed a preacher, an apostle, and a <mark class="chg">teacher.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Σὺ οὖν κακοπάθησον; 개역개정&#x27;s 나와 함께 renders the critical συγκακοπαθησον, which the TR lacks" data-ref="2 Timothy 2:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 2:3</span><span class="srnote">TR Σὺ οὖν κακοπάθησον; 개역개정's 나와 함께 renders the critical συγκακοπαθησον, which the TR lacks</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">You therefore must endure hardship as a good soldier of Jesus Christ.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">You therefore must endure hardship <mark class="chg">with me</mark> as a good soldier of Jesus Christ.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ὄνομα Χριστοῦ (name of Christ), not κυρίου (주의 이름)" data-ref="2 Timothy 2:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 2:19</span><span class="srnote">TR ὄνομα Χριστοῦ (name of Christ), not κυρίου (주의 이름)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Nevertheless the solid foundation of God stands, having this seal: "The Lord knows those who are His," and, "Let everyone who names the name of <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> depart from iniquity."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Nevertheless the solid foundation of God stands, having this seal: "The Lord knows those who are His," and, "Let everyone who names the name of <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> depart from iniquity."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has 그리스도 예수, dropping 주" data-ref="2 Timothy 4:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 4:1</span><span class="srnote">TR τοῦ Κυρίου Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ; 개역개정 has 그리스도 예수, dropping 주</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I charge you therefore before God and <mark class="chg">the Lord Jesus Christ,</mark> who will judge the living and the dead at His appearing and His kingdom:</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I charge you therefore before God and <mark class="chg">Christ Jesus,</mark> who will judge the living and the dead at His appearing and His kingdom:</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Ὁ Κύριος Ἰησοῦς Χριστὸς and a closing Ἀμήν; 개역개정 has 주께서 only and no 아멘" data-ref="2 Timothy 4:22"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">2 Timothy 4:22</span><span class="srnote">TR Ὁ Κύριος Ἰησοῦς Χριστὸς and a closing Ἀμήν; 개역개정 has 주께서 only and no 아멘</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The Lord <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ</mark> be with your spirit. Grace be with you. <mark class="chg">Amen.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The Lord be with your spirit. Grace be with you.</span></span></tr>
