Amen.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds ἀρχὴ καὶ τέλος (beginning and end)" data-ref="Revelation 1:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 1:8</span><span class="srnote">TR adds ἀρχὴ καὶ τέλος (beginning and end)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">"I am the Alpha and the <mark class="chg">Omega, the Beginning and the End,"</mark> says the Lord, "who is and who was and who is to come, the Almighty."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">"I am the Alpha and the <mark class="chg">Omega,"</mark> says the Lord, "who is and who was and who is to come, the Almighty."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ in both places; 개역개정 has 예수 only" data-ref="Revelation 1:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 1:9</span><span class="srnote">TR reads Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ in both places; 개역개정 has 예수 only</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I, John, both your brother and companion in the tribulation and kingdom and patience of <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ,</mark> was on the island that is called Patmos for the word of God and for the testimony of <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I, John, both your brother and companion in the tribulation and kingdom and patience of <mark class="chg">Jesus,</mark> was on the island that is called Patmos for the word of God and for the testimony of <mark class="chg">Jesus.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds Ἐγώ εἰμι τὸ ἄλφα καὶ τὸ ὦ, ὁ πρῶτος καὶ ὁ ἔσχατος and ταῖς ἐν Ἀσίᾳ" data-ref="Revelation 1:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 1:11</span><span class="srnote">TR adds Ἐγώ εἰμι τὸ ἄλφα καὶ τὸ ὦ, ὁ πρῶτος καὶ ὁ ἔσχατος and ταῖς ἐν Ἀσίᾳ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">saying, <mark class="chg">"I am the Alpha and the Omega, the First and the Last," and,</mark> "What you see, write in a book and send it to the seven <mark class="chg">churches which are in Asia:</mark> to Ephesus, Smyrna, Pergamos, Thyatira, Sardis, Philadelphia, and Laodicea."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">saying, "What you see, write in a book and send it to the seven <mark class="chg">churches:</mark> to Ephesus, Smyrna, Pergamos, Thyatira, Sardis, Philadelphia, and Laodicea."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἔρχομαί σοι ταχύ (I will come to you quickly)" data-ref="Revelation 2:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 2:5</span><span class="srnote">TR ἔρχομαί σοι ταχύ (I will come to you quickly)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Remember therefore from where you have fallen; repent and do the first works, or else I will come to you <mark class="chg">quickly</mark> and remove your lampstand from its place, unless you repent.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Remember therefore from where you have fallen; repent and do the first works, or else I will come to you and remove your lampstand from its place, unless you repent.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Οἶδά σου τὰ ἔργα καὶ τὴν θλῖψιν (I know your works and tribulation)" data-ref="Revelation 2:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 2:9</span><span class="srnote">TR Οἶδά σου τὰ ἔργα καὶ τὴν θλῖψιν (I know your works and tribulation)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I know your <mark class="chg">works, and tribulation,</mark> and poverty (but you are rich), and the blasphemy of those who say they are Jews and are not, but are a synagogue of Satan.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I know your <mark class="chg">tribulation</mark> and poverty (but you are rich), and the blasphemy of those who say they are Jews and are not, but are a synagogue of Satan.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR Οἶδα τὰ ἔργα σου καὶ ποῦ κατοικεῖς (your works and where you dwell)" data-ref="Revelation 2:13"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 2:13</span><span class="srnote">TR Οἶδα τὰ ἔργα σου καὶ ποῦ κατοικεῖς (your works and where you dwell)</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">I know <mark class="chg">your works, and</mark> where you dwell, where Satan's throne is. And you hold fast to My name, and did not deny My faith even in the days in which Antipas was My faithful martyr, who was killed among you, where Satan dwells.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">I know where you dwell, where Satan's throne is. And you hold fast to My name, and did not deny My faith even in the days in which Antipas was My faithful martyr, who was killed among you, where Satan dwells.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἠγόρασας τῷ Θεῷ ἡμᾶς (you redeemed us); 개역개정&#x27;s 사람들을 follows the critical text" data-ref="Revelation 5:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 5:9</span><span class="srnote">TR ἠγόρασας τῷ Θεῷ ἡμᾶς (you redeemed us); 개역개정's 사람들을 follows the critical text</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And they sang a new song, saying: You are worthy to take the scroll, and to open its seals; for You were slain, and have redeemed <mark class="chg">us</mark> for God by Your blood out of every tribe and tongue and people and nation,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And they sang a new song, saying: You are worthy to take the scroll, and to open its seals; for You were slain, and have redeemed <mark class="chg">people</mark> for God by Your blood out of every tribe and tongue and people and nation,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ἐποίησας αὐτοὺς βασιλεῖς καὶ ἱερεῖς (them kings and priests), not βασιλείαν" data-ref="Revelation 5:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 5:10</span><span class="srnote">TR ἐποίησας αὐτοὺς βασιλεῖς καὶ ἱερεῖς (them kings and priests), not βασιλείαν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">and have made them <mark class="chg">kings</mark> and priests to our God, and they shall reign on the earth.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">and have made them <mark class="chg">a kingdom</mark> and priests to our God, and they shall reign on the earth.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR omits καὶ τὸ τρίτον τῆς γῆς κατεκάη, the critical text&#x27;s third of the earth" data-ref="Revelation 8:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 8:7</span><span class="srnote">TR omits καὶ τὸ τρίτον τῆς γῆς κατεκάη, the critical text's third of the earth</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-add" data-k="drop">added</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The first angel sounded: And hail and fire followed, mingled with blood, and they were thrown to the earth. And a third of the trees was burned up, and all green grass was burned up.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The first angel sounded: And hail and fire followed, mingled with blood, and they were thrown to the earth. And a third of the <mark class="chg">earth was burned up, and a third of the</mark> trees was burned up, and all green grass was burned up.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds καὶ ὁ ἄγγελος εἱστήκει (and the angel stood) before λέγων; 개역개정 has no angel" data-ref="Revelation 11:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 11:1</span><span class="srnote">TR adds καὶ ὁ ἄγγελος εἱστήκει (and the angel stood) before λέγων; 개역개정 has no angel</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then I was given a reed like a measuring rod. And <mark class="chg">the angel stood, saying:</mark> Rise and measure the temple of God, the altar, and those who worship there.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then I was given a reed like a measuring rod. And <mark class="chg">I was told:</mark> Rise and measure the temple of God, the altar, and those who worship there.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὁ ὢν καὶ ὁ ἦν καὶ ὁ ἐρχόμενος (who is and was and is to come); 개역개정 drops ὁ ἐρχόμενος" data-ref="Revelation 11:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 11:17</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὁ ὢν καὶ ὁ ἦν καὶ ὁ ἐρχόμενος (who is and was and is to come); 개역개정 drops ὁ ἐρχόμενος</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">saying: We give You thanks, O Lord God Almighty, the One who is and who <mark class="chg">was and who is to come,</mark> because You have taken Your great power and reigned.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">saying: We give You thanks, O Lord God Almighty, the One who is and who <mark class="chg">was,</mark> because You have taken Your great power and reigned.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR adds Χριστοῦ; &#x27;stood on the sand&#x27; belongs to 13:1 in this TR edition" data-ref="Revelation 12:17"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 12:17</span><span class="srnote">TR adds Χριστοῦ; 'stood on the sand' belongs to 13:1 in this TR edition</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And the dragon was enraged with the woman, and he went to make war with the rest of her offspring, who keep the commandments of God and have the testimony of <mark class="chg">Jesus Christ.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And the dragon was enraged with the woman, and he went to make war with the rest of her offspring, who keep the commandments of God and have the testimony of <mark class="chg">Jesus. And he stood on the sand of the sea.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens 13:1 with καὶ ἐστάθην ἐπὶ τὴν ἄμμον τῆς θαλάσσης" data-ref="Revelation 13:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 13:1</span><span class="srnote">TR opens 13:1 with καὶ ἐστάθην ἐπὶ τὴν ἄμμον τῆς θαλάσσης</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then <mark class="chg">I stood on the sand of the sea. And</mark> I saw a beast rising up out of the sea, having ten horns and seven heads, and on his horns ten crowns, and on his heads a blasphemous name.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then I saw a beast rising up out of the sea, having ten horns and seven heads, and on his horns ten crowns, and on his heads a blasphemous name.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR closes ἄμωμοι γάρ εἰσιν ἐνώπιον τοῦ θρόνου τοῦ Θεοῦ (before the throne of God); 개역개정 omits the clause" data-ref="Revelation 14:5"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 14:5</span><span class="srnote">TR closes ἄμωμοι γάρ εἰσιν ἐνώπιον τοῦ θρόνου τοῦ Θεοῦ (before the throne of God); 개역개정 omits the clause</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And in their mouth was found no deceit, for they are without <mark class="chg">fault before the throne of God.</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And in their mouth was found no deceit, for they are without <mark class="chg">fault.</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR has four objects, including ἐκ τοῦ χαράγματος αὐτοῦ (his mark); 개역개정 lists only three" data-ref="Revelation 15:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 15:2</span><span class="srnote">TR has four objects, including ἐκ τοῦ χαράγματος αὐτοῦ (his mark); 개역개정 lists only three</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And I saw something like a sea of glass mingled with fire, and those who have the victory over the beast, over his image and over <mark class="chg">his mark and over</mark> the number of his name, standing on the sea of glass, having harps of God.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And I saw something like a sea of glass mingled with fire, and those who have the victory over the beast, over his image and over the number of his name, standing on the sea of glass, having harps of God.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ὁ βασιλεὺς τῶν ἁγίων (King of saints), not τῶν ἐθνῶν/αἰώνων that 개역개정&#x27;s 만국 reflects" data-ref="Revelation 15:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 15:3</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ὁ βασιλεὺς τῶν ἁγίων (King of saints), not τῶν ἐθνῶν/αἰώνων that 개역개정's 만국 reflects</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">They sing the song of Moses, the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb, saying: "Great and marvelous are Your works, Lord God Almighty! Just and true are Your ways, O King of <mark class="chg">saints!"</mark></span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">They sing the song of Moses, the servant of God, and the song of the Lamb, saying: "Great and marvelous are Your works, Lord God Almighty! Just and true are Your ways, O King of <mark class="chg">the nations!"</mark></span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR ends the description καίπερ ἔστιν (and yet is); 개역개정 stops at 지금은 없으나" data-ref="Revelation 17:8"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 17:8</span><span class="srnote">TR ends the description καίπερ ἔστιν (and yet is); 개역개정 stops at 지금은 없으나</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The beast that you saw was, and is not, and will ascend out of the bottomless pit and go to perdition. And those who dwell on the earth, whose names have not been written in the Book of Life from the foundation of the world, will marvel when they see the beast that was, and is not, and <mark class="chg">yet is, and</mark> will come.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The beast that you saw was, and is not, and will ascend out of the bottomless pit and go to perdition. And those who dwell on the earth, whose names have not been written in the Book of Life from the foundation of the world, will marvel when they see the beast that was, and is not, and will come.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads πέπωκε πάντα τὰ ἔθνη (all nations have drunk); 개역개정&#x27;s 무너졌으며 follows the critical πέπτωκαν" data-ref="Revelation 18:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 18:3</span><span class="srnote">TR reads πέπωκε πάντα τὰ ἔθνη (all nations have drunk); 개역개정's 무너졌으며 follows the critical πέπτωκαν</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">For all nations have <mark class="chg">drunk of</mark> the wine of the wrath of her fornication, and the kings of the earth have committed fornication with her, and the merchants of the earth have become rich through the abundance of her luxury.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">For all nations have <mark class="chg">fallen by</mark> the wine of the wrath of her fornication, and the kings of the earth have committed fornication with her, and the merchants of the earth have become rich through the abundance of her luxury.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR keeps the second person singular οὐ μὴ εὑρήσῃς αὐτά, addressed to Babylon; 개역개정 turns it into a statement about people" data-ref="Revelation 18:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 18:14</span><span class="srnote">TR keeps the second person singular οὐ μὴ εὑρήσῃς αὐτά, addressed to Babylon; 개역개정 turns it into a statement about people</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The fruit that your soul longed for has gone from you, and all the things which are rich and splendid have gone from you, and <mark class="chg">you</mark> will never see them again.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The fruit that your soul longed for has gone from you, and all the things which are rich and splendid have gone from you, and <mark class="chg">people</mark> will never see them again.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἡ σωτηρία καὶ ἡ δόξα καὶ ἡ τιμὴ καὶ ἡ δύναμις Κυρίῳ τῷ Θεῷ ἡμῶν; 개역개정 drops τιμή and 주" data-ref="Revelation 19:1"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 19:1</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἡ σωτηρία καὶ ἡ δόξα καὶ ἡ τιμὴ καὶ ἡ δύναμις Κυρίῳ τῷ Θεῷ ἡμῶν; 개역개정 drops τιμή and 주</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">After these things I heard a loud voice of a great multitude in heaven, saying: Alleluia! Salvation and glory and <mark class="chg">honor and</mark> power belong to <mark class="chg">the Lord</mark> our God!</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">After these things I heard a loud voice of a great multitude in heaven, saying: Alleluia! Salvation and glory and power belong to our God!</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads κατέβη πῦρ ἀπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ; 개역개정 omits ἀπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ" data-ref="Revelation 20:9"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 20:9</span><span class="srnote">TR reads κατέβη πῦρ ἀπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ; 개역개정 omits ἀπὸ τοῦ Θεοῦ</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">They went up on the breadth of the earth and surrounded the camp of the saints and the beloved city. And fire came down from <mark class="chg">God out of</mark> heaven and devoured them.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">They went up on the breadth of the earth and surrounded the camp of the saints and the beloved city. And fire came down from heaven and devoured them.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἑστῶτας ἐνώπιον τοῦ Θεοῦ; 개역개정&#x27;s 보좌 앞 follows the critical τοῦ θρόνου" data-ref="Revelation 20:12"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 20:12</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἑστῶτας ἐνώπιον τοῦ Θεοῦ; 개역개정's 보좌 앞 follows the critical τοῦ θρόνου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And I saw the dead, small and great, standing before <mark class="chg">God,</mark> and books were opened. And another book was opened, which is the Book of Life. And the dead were judged according to their works, by the things which were written in the books.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And I saw the dead, small and great, standing before <mark class="chg">the throne,</mark> and books were opened. And another book was opened, which is the Book of Life. And the dead were judged according to their works, by the things which were written in the books.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR opens Καὶ ἐγὼ Ἰωάννης εἶδον (and I, John, saw); 개역개정 keeps only 내가" data-ref="Revelation 21:2"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 21:2</span><span class="srnote">TR opens Καὶ ἐγὼ Ἰωάννης εἶδον (and I, John, saw); 개역개정 keeps only 내가</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then <mark class="chg">I, John,</mark> saw the holy city, New Jerusalem, coming down out of heaven from God, prepared as a bride adorned for her husband.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then <mark class="chg">I</mark> saw the holy city, New Jerusalem, coming down out of heaven from God, prepared as a bride adorned for her husband.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ (from heaven); 개역개정&#x27;s 보좌에서 follows the critical ἐκ τοῦ θρόνου" data-ref="Revelation 21:3"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 21:3</span><span class="srnote">TR reads ἐκ τοῦ οὐρανοῦ (from heaven); 개역개정's 보좌에서 follows the critical ἐκ τοῦ θρόνου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And I heard a loud voice from <mark class="chg">heaven</mark> saying, "Behold, the tabernacle of God is with men, and He will dwell with them, and they shall be His people, and God Himself will be with them."</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And I heard a loud voice from <mark class="chg">the throne</mark> saying, "Behold, the tabernacle of God is with men, and He will dwell with them, and they shall be His people, and God Himself will be with them."</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads κληρονομήσει πάντα (inherit all things); 개역개정&#x27;s 이것들을 follows the critical ταῦτα" data-ref="Revelation 21:7"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 21:7</span><span class="srnote">TR reads κληρονομήσει πάντα (inherit all things); 개역개정's 이것들을 follows the critical ταῦτα</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He who overcomes shall inherit <mark class="chg">all</mark> things, and I will be his God and he shall be My son.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He who overcomes shall inherit <mark class="chg">these</mark> things, and I will be his God and he shall be My son.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τὴν πόλιν τὴν μεγάλην, τὴν ἁγίαν Ἱερουσαλήμ (the great city, the holy Jerusalem); 개역개정 drops μεγάλην" data-ref="Revelation 21:10"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 21:10</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τὴν πόλιν τὴν μεγάλην, τὴν ἁγίαν Ἱερουσαλήμ (the great city, the holy Jerusalem); 개역개정 drops μεγάλην</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And he carried me away in the Spirit to a great and high mountain, and showed me the <mark class="chg">great city, the</mark> holy Jerusalem, descending out of heaven from God,</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And he carried me away in the Spirit to a great and high mountain, and showed me the holy <mark class="chg">city,</mark> Jerusalem, descending out of heaven from God,</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads τὰ ἔθνη τῶν σῳζομένων … φέρουσιν τὴν δόξαν καὶ τὴν τιμήν; 개역개정 drops both 구원받은 자들의 and 존귀" data-ref="Revelation 21:24"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 21:24</span><span class="srnote">TR reads τὰ ἔθνη τῶν σῳζομένων … φέρουσιν τὴν δόξαν καὶ τὴν τιμήν; 개역개정 drops both 구원받은 자들의 and 존귀</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">And the nations <mark class="chg">of those who are saved</mark> shall walk in its light, and the kings of the earth bring their glory <mark class="chg">and honor</mark> into it.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">And the nations shall walk in its light, and the kings of the earth bring their glory into it.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads των αγιων προφητων (God of the holy prophets); 개역개정 follows the critical text&#x27;s των πνευματων των προφητων, so 영의 must go and 거룩한 come in" data-ref="Revelation 22:6"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:6</span><span class="srnote">TR reads των αγιων προφητων (God of the holy prophets); 개역개정 follows the critical text's των πνευματων των προφητων, so 영의 must go and 거룩한 come in</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Then he said to me, "These words are faithful and true." And the Lord God of the <mark class="chg">holy</mark> prophets sent His angel to show His servants the things which must shortly take place.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Then he said to me, "These words are faithful and true." And the Lord God of the <mark class="chg">spirits of the</mark> prophets sent His angel to show His servants the things which must shortly take place.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR δικαιωθήτω (&#x27;let him be righteous still&#x27;), not the critical δικαιοσύνην ποιησάτω" data-ref="Revelation 22:11"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:11</span><span class="srnote">TR δικαιωθήτω ('let him be righteous still'), not the critical δικαιοσύνην ποιησάτω</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">He who is unjust, let him be unjust still; he who is filthy, let him be filthy still; he who is righteous, let him <mark class="chg">be righteous</mark> still; he who is holy, let him be holy still.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">He who is unjust, let him be unjust still; he who is filthy, let him be filthy still; he who is righteous, let him <mark class="chg">practice righteousness</mark> still; he who is holy, let him be holy still.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads οι ποιουντες τας εντολας αυτου (those who do His commandments); 개역개정 follows the critical text&#x27;s οι πλυνοντες τας στολας αυτων (those who wash their robes), a wholly different beatitude" data-ref="Revelation 22:14"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:14</span><span class="srnote">TR reads οι ποιουντες τας εντολας αυτου (those who do His commandments); 개역개정 follows the critical text's οι πλυνοντες τας στολας αυτων (those who wash their robes), a wholly different beatitude</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">Blessed are those who <mark class="chg">do His commandments,</mark> that they may have the right to the tree of life, and may enter through the gates into the city.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">Blessed are those who <mark class="chg">wash their robes,</mark> that they may have the right to the tree of life, and may enter through the gates into the city.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads απο Βιβλου της ζωης (from the Book of Life), not the critical text&#x27;s απο του ξυλου της ζωης (from the tree of life) — 생명나무 would teach the wrong thing. TR also lists three separate losses: the book of life, the holy city, and what is written in this book" data-ref="Revelation 22:19"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:19</span><span class="srnote">TR reads απο Βιβλου της ζωης (from the Book of Life), not the critical text's απο του ξυλου της ζωης (from the tree of life) — 생명나무 would teach the wrong thing. TR also lists three separate losses: the book of life, the holy city, and what is written in this book</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-chg" data-k="chg">changed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">If anyone takes away from the words of the book of this prophecy, God shall take away his part <mark class="chg">from</mark> the <mark class="chg">Book</mark> of <mark class="chg">Life, from</mark> the holy city, <mark class="chg">and from the things</mark> which are written in this book.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">If anyone takes away from the words of the book of this prophecy, God shall take away his part <mark class="chg">in</mark> the <mark class="chg">tree</mark> of <mark class="chg">life and in</mark> the holy city, which are written in this book.</span></span></tr>
<tr class="tr" data-note="TR reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου (our Lord Jesus Christ); 개역개정&#x27;s 주 예수 drops both ημων and Χριστου" data-ref="Revelation 22:21"><td class="c-ref"><span class="rf">Revelation 22:21</span><span class="srnote">TR reads του Κυριου ημων Ιησου Χριστου (our Lord Jesus Christ); 개역개정's 주 예수 drops both ημων and Χριστου</span></td><td class="c-kind"><span class="k k-drop" data-k="add">removed</span></td><td class="c-text"><span class="ln tr"><span class="tx">The grace of <mark class="chg">our</mark> Lord Jesus <mark class="chg">Christ</mark> be with all. Amen.</span></span><span class="ln ct"><span class="tx">The grace of <mark class="chg">the</mark> Lord Jesus be with all. Amen.</span></span></tr>
</tbody></table></div>

The nine places a critical text still brackets outright (Mark 16:9–20, John 7:53–8:11 and the rest) are a different question, so they are not in this table. Why any of this happened, and why I read an NKJV, is in [the essay](/nkjv/).
